- **Checked** (2026-11-06): in the container `just ci-lint ci-test ci-vuln` and `ci-licenses` with the list of `go.yml` pass; the profile is one line `mode: atomic`; govulncheck — "No vulnerabilities found"; `ci-licenses` without the list fails and says where to get it; `ci-lint` fails on a file that `gofmt` would rewrite. `gh release verify v0.1.15 -R altnovatrail/.github` — "Release v0.1.15 verified!". The run of the job on a pull request — after the author's push.

— *Why:* without the module, the recipes and the job "Go" the code of T13–T20 has nowhere to pass its checks, while tests and `codecov/patch` come with every change of code (RULES.md, How we work; R48 spec). What the code adds to the files of CI is written by the repository's own session (the 2026-10-21 amendment to R30 spec). The references are all to v0.1.15: `codeql.yml` takes `go` only in the new releases, `go.yml` with the coverage upload is from v0.1.12, while `secrets.yml`, `dco.yml`, `documents.yml` and `scorecard.yml` did not change between v0.1.0 and v0.1.15, so raising them changes one SHA, and Dependabot's pull requests #5–#9 will close by themselves. It costs no money: the repository is public (R30, R48 spec).

**R14. gopls stands in the devcontainer image at an exact version, v0.24.0, and is built from its own lock file `.devcontainer/gopls/`, as in R19 module-kit. This is a difference of `learner-model`'s container from `spec`'s container until `spec`'s template gives gopls to the kind `go`.** The author's answer of 2026-11-06 in planning T12 (RUN.md, T12, «Решено автором»).
- **What is set up** — the same as in `module-kit`, word for word, except the repository's name and the entry's number:
  - `.devcontainer/gopls/go.mod` — the module `devcontainer/gopls` with `tool golang.org/x/tools/gopls`, and `go.sum` holds the hash of every module of the build. `go mod init` and `go get -tool golang.org/x/tools/gopls@v0.24.0` gave them; `go.sum` matched `module-kit`'s `go.sum` byte for byte, and `go mod verify` — "all modules verified";
  - in the stage `devcontainer` of the Dockerfile — `ARG GOPLS_VERSION=0.24.0`, a `COPY` of the lock file and `go install -mod=readonly tool` into `/usr/local/bin`; `gopls version` must name `GOPLS_VERSION` as a whole line. The stage `toolchain`, in which the checks run, does not change;
  - `devcontainer.json` — the Go extension does not update its tools, does not look for their updates and does not lint on save, and the comment to `context` says what the image takes from its folder `gopls/`. A missing tool — the debugger dlv, say — it still offers to install, without an exact version, and such an offer is declined;
  - `ci-lint` checks `GOPLS_VERSION` against the version in the lock file: CI does not build the stage `devcontainer`;
  - `dependabot.yml` does not take the folder `.devcontainer/gopls/`: gopls is raised by `go get -tool` in it together with `GOPLS_VERSION`.
- **Checked** (2026-11-06): the lock file in a temporary folder builds gopls by the same `go install -mod=readonly tool`, and `gopls version` is `golang.org/x/tools/gopls v0.24.0`; `ci-lint` fails and names both versions with `GOPLS_VERSION=0.24.1` and without `ARG GOPLS_VERSION`. There is no Docker in the container, and the image was not built here: the author's Rebuild Container will check it.

— *Why:* without gopls in the image the Go extension installs its latest version by itself, not an exact one (RULES.md, Exact versions only). `spec` has already decided to give the kind `go` the same step (R102 spec, the answer to В19 module-kit), but the epic E7 that will do it came out neither in v0.9.3 nor in v0.9.4 (`gh api repos/altnovatrail/spec/compare/v0.9.3...v0.9.4`, 2026-11-06). gopls v0.24.0 is the latest, of 2026-10-14 ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 4); the license is BSD-3-Clause, and it is a development tool: it is shipped nowhere and puts none of its own code into what ships.

— *Rejected:*
- waiting for `spec`'s template: until the tag with E7 the Go extension would install gopls at an unpinned version;
- only forbidding the extension to install tools: no language server until the same tag.

— *Consequence:* the Dockerfile and `devcontainer.json` no longer match `spec`'s recipe byte for byte. The move to the tag of `spec` where the template gives gopls to the kind `go` removes the step of its own and replaces this entry — Д12 in table 4 of RUN.md. Rebuild Container is the author's step. — `.devcontainer/`, `justfile` (`ci-lint`), `.github/dependabot.yml`; CLAUDE.md; RUN.md, T12, table 4.

— *Superseded 2026-11-09:* by entry R19 — since `spec` v0.9.16 the step of gopls and the lock file are given to the kind `go` by `spec`'s template.

## The `spec` tag v0.9.5

**R15. `learner-model` stands on `spec` v0.9.5 — the latest release. The tag records `spec`'s answers to both rows «в реестр spec», В23 and В24, by their recommendations, and the rows are closed. It also asks of `Read` two signs of the module's trial series (R104 spec), and that goes into T20.** The author's decision of 2026-11-07 — move to the latest tag; a task outside an epic, T24 of RUN.md.
- **What the tag brings `learner-model`** (`git -C spec diff v0.9.3 v0.9.5`, read 2026-11-07; Д111 and Д112 in table 4 of `spec`'s RUN.md):
  - the answer to В24 (Д111) — R103 spec, which closes Т-16 too:
    - the model's first version is the baseline before the first tag of `sim`, released signed. R103 spec split row 4.6 of `COORDINATION.md` into 4.6–4.8;
    - the gate: the library's CI takes `sim` by its exact tag and the SHA of its commit, in a job of its own on `ubuntu-24.04` with `timeout-minutes: 15`, in the stage `toolchain` of the devcontainer of `sim` at the same tag. It writes `go.work` on the fly, without committing it: `sim`, the pull request's code and `engine` — by the commit from the gate file of `sim`. The gate runs on every pull request and every commit of `main` and holds the merge;
    - the order of releases: a change that breaks the harness's API or moves the numbers is first recorded in `sim` at the commit of its pull request; a tag of `sim` comes out; the model's pull request raises it and is merged; after the model's release `sim` raises `go.mod`. The pair of the threshold "mastered" and the target of the corridor, and a change of the model that breaks the API of `engine`, go in one tag of `sim`: the gate file names both commits, the model is merged when the pull request of `engine` has passed review, and `api` moves to both versions together.

    This is the 2026-11-07 amendment to R12, T21 and T22;
  - the answer to В23 — the 2026-11-07 addition to О-25, by option 1. An answer in the second way is evidence with the weight of a handwritten answer; a submission whose verdict the learner saw the model always counts; one cancelled before the verdict does not reach evidence. The card architecture 3.8 spec says the same. It needs no fields of evidence and no code — subsections 2.7 and 3.3 of [architecture.md](architecture.md);
  - R104 spec (Д112): `Read` gives for a module whether its trial series has ended and whether the module's last piece of evidence was still in it — so that the engine does not work through an error or give probes within the series, nor work through the error of the series' last answer either. The model counts the series, and the engine does not repeat its rules. The output type of `Read` since T15 carries no such signs, and the state does not remember whether the last piece of evidence was still in the series. This is T20, and how the state learns it is decided in its planning;
  - concepts 15 spec — "virtual learner" and "population": `sim` has populations, not profiles, because a profile is a learner in an account. The card architecture 3.9 spec calls the detailing of `sim` finished (R03–R05 sim);
  - the rest between the tags — `COORDINATION.md`, the 2026-11-06 and 2026-11-07 amendments to R93 spec about the sample file and the recipe `sample-check`, the examples of eval contracts — does not concern `learner-model`. The template, `.devcontainer/`, RULES.md and the documents check did not change.
- **What is changed here:**
  - `spec/` — to v0.9.5, commit 86f442a; the tag in CLAUDE.md, README.md and at the start of RUN.md;
  - the template and `.devcontainer/` — unchanged: `git -C spec diff v0.9.3 v0.9.5` does not touch `repo-template/` and `.devcontainer/`, so a three-way merge gives nothing, and the image is the same;
  - CLAUDE.md and README.md — [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 2, no longer has an open question, and the first version comes out before the first tag of `sim`. README.md — also the state of the code: T16 did not update it, and it said there was no code of the model, though since T16 there are the level and "mastered". The edit is in the same paragraph as the answer to О-25;
  - [architecture.md](architecture.md):
    - section 1 — the gate by R103 spec and the populations, and the first version comes out before the first tag of `sim`;
    - section 2 — subsection 2.7 is decided;
    - 3.1 and 3.7 — the signs of the trial series in `Read`;

    - 3.3 — the way of input does not change the weight;
  - RUN.md:

    - T21 and T22 no longer have "the answer to В24";

The answers to В23 and В24 exist only in `spec`: for В24, Д111 says to close it by the move; В23 `spec` recorded as a row of `learner-model` in table 5 of its RUN.md and as an addition to О-25; and Д112 brings the signs of the series into `Read`. ## The first release

**R16. The release of `learner-model` is made by the shared `release.yml` of `.github` on the line `0.1`, starting with `v0.1.0`, when a commit of `main` with green CI changes the code, the schema or what the file is built with. Go consumers take the code as a Go module by tag, and the release's only file is the schema archive `learner-model-proto-<version>.tar.gz`, signed keylessly; `spec`'s `release-verify` checks it. Immutable releases and a ruleset of the tags `v*` are turned on before the first release, and the control of the rules test is no longer a nested module, so that the Go module zip carries the whole test suite.** The author's answers of 2026-11-07 in planning T21 (RUN.md), all three by the plan's recommendations.
- **What is decided:**
  - **the release file** is one: the schema archive `learner-model-proto-<version>.tar.gz`. In it is a folder of the same name with `LICENSE`, `buf.yaml` and all the `.proto` from `proto/`, in a tar that every build writes the same way: names in order, without times and owners, mode 0644, gzip without a name and a time. This is the description of the form of evidence and state for those who read them without Go — for the export of a learner's data (the note on epic E4 in table 2 of `spec`'s RUN.md; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.5, "Export");
  - **the code** — as the Go module `github.com/altnovatrail/learner-model` by the release's tag, through the proxy and the checksum database, as the module of `spec` (R64 spec): `go get github.com/altnovatrail/learner-model@v0.1.0`. It is not a release file, and `release-verify` does not touch it, while `go.sum` and sum.golang.org hold it;
  - **the control of the rules test** keeps its `go.mod` under the name `go.mod.control`, and the rules test reads it under that name. The control's `go.sum` is deleted: it was needed only by SonarCloud, whose rule S8566 looks at a `go.mod` with `require`. `TestNoNestedModules` holds that there is one nested module below the root — the lock file of gopls `.devcontainer/gopls` (R14), which neither the code nor a test reads —, while the zip carries neither the submodule `spec` nor the folders of version control systems anyway;
  - **the settings** were turned on by the executor through `gh api`, before the merge of T21's pull request: immutable releases — `enabled: true` — and the ruleset `v-tags`, id 26770474: `refs/tags/v*`, `update`, `deletion`, `non_fast_forward`, without bypass, a copy of `module-kit`'s 25559103. Checked against the API's answers 2026-11-07.
- **A proposal of the plan and of the execution, not the author's answer:**
  - **`release.yml`** — as in `module-kit`: `workflow_run` of CI on `main` and a button, permissions `contents: write`, `id-token: write`, `actions: read`, the shared workflow v0.1.15 by SHA. `RELEASE_LINE` is `0.1`: the line's first release is `v0.1.0`, as R12 named it, and before v1.0.0 the API may change;
  - **`paths`** — the code without tests and `testdata/`, `go.mod`, `proto/`, `buf.yaml`, `LICENSE`, `justfile`, `.devcontainer/Dockerfile` and `release.yml` itself. Tests, golden files and documents give no release: the module does not change by them for whoever imports it;
  - **the recipes** — `release-files <version>`, which the shared release calls, and `ci-release` in the job "Proto": two builds — the same bytes, the archive's content — `LICENSE`, `buf.yaml` and all the `.proto` git knows, as in the tree, and `buf build` from the unpacked archive. `ci-release` does not run beside a ready `dist/`: `release-files` would replace it;
  - **`buf breaking`** from the first tag checks the schema against it — `ci-proto` takes the latest tag `v*`, as the 2026-11-07 amendment to R10 decided.

— *Why:*
- **A Go module, not a file.** `sim`, `engine` and `api` are code in Go, and `go.mod` with `go.sum` pin the version themselves, while the checksum database keeps the hash of the zip forever. The module of `spec` is already taken so (R64 spec), and a second route would only split the version in two.
- **The file is the schema.** The shared release needs at least one file in `dist/`, and the schema is the only thing needed without Go: for the export, so that the log can be read without the library (R09 spec; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.5). The signature and the attestation of an immutable release link it with the tag's commit (R77 spec).
- **A reproducible tar.** The bytes do not depend on the build's time and owner, so the file can be built again from the tag and compared, and `ci-release` holds this from every pull request.
- **A zip without nested modules.** "Module zip files do not include the contents of vendor directories or any nested modules (subdirectories that contain go.mod files)" (Go Modules Reference, Module zip files; the quote of R64 spec). The control with `go.mod` would not get into the zip, and `TestCodeRulesControl` would fail in the consumer's module cache, while the zip of a version can no longer be fixed.
- **The settings before the first release** are the condition of the check of R77 spec: "a repository turns them on before its first release".

— *Rejected:*
- an archive of the module's whole source — it repeats the Go module, which consumers take by tag anyway; both archives;
- leaving the control a nested module and recording a debt — v0.1.0 would forever carry a failing test;
- turning on the settings by the author's hands — the executor turns them on and checks them against the API's answer, as the ruleset `main` in R13;
- a trial run of the shared release with `publish: false` on a pull request — `ci-release` holds the build of the file, and the shared release checks the signature itself after publishing;
- tests and `testdata/` in `paths` — the module zip carries them too, but a release on every edit of a test or a golden file would give consumers versions in which nothing changed for import. An edit of a test comes out with the next release of code, as in `module-kit`, whose `paths` are also without `*_test.go`.

— *Consequence:* RUN.md, T21 — the first release comes out by the merge of its pull request, and it is irreversible: the merge comes with a separate confirmation of the author; the outcome, the checks and the row «в реестр spec» about the released version — by a second pull request. [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.6 and 3.7; README — the section on releases. — `.github/workflows/release.yml`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `justfile`, `rules_test.go`, `testdata/coderules/`.

— *Amendment 2026-11-07:* so it came out. Pull request #30 was merged with the author's confirmation, d2620a1, and the run "Release" released `v0.1.0` on d2620a1 — the release is immutable, with the file `learner-model-proto-v0.1.0.tar.gz` and its `.sigstore.json`. `gh release verify v0.1.0` accepts the attestation, `spec`'s `release-verify` the file: "signed by the shared release.yml of .github v0.1.15", and `just release-files v0.1.0` from d2620a1 gives the same bytes. `proxy.golang.org` serves the Go module with the sum `h1:KK3u66KFWfWV8NmI+ueCXZQVXyUrg8zfDtiyo8+Me9Q=` and Origin `refs/tags/v0.1.0`; its zip holds `testdata/coderules/go.mod.control` and only the root `go.mod`, and `go test github.com/altnovatrail/learner-model/...` from a module that requires it is green, the rules test with its control and `TestNoNestedModules` too. `ci-proto` now checks `buf breaking` against `v0.1.0`. - **What this is.** R107 spec made the checks that R09 held as its own shared by `learner-model` and `engine`: the runs on the targets and `fma-diff` from `.github` v0.1.18, the job arm64 from v0.1.19, the code rules test — as the tool `tools/coderules/` with the caller's lists — from v0.1.20 (R16 and R33 .github). The tool is a superset of the copies: beyond the rules of `rules_test.go` it holds `replace` in `go.mod` and nested modules by the list, and its control for `learner-model` is `tools/coderules/testdata/learner-model`, the same module as the former `testdata/coderules`, except the comments (`diff -r`, 2026-11-08).
- **The lists** (`.github/coderules.json`): the code's imports — the former list of [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.6, without `math/bits`, which no file imports; the tests' imports — `bytes`, `crypto/sha256`, `fmt`, `math/rand/v2`, `os`, `path/filepath`, `runtime` and `testing`, without `go/token`, `go/parser`, `go/ast`, `go/types` and `go/importer`, which only the rules test needed. `runtime` is needed by the tests of `detmath`: in wasm and on 386 the port is compared with `math` bit for bit; the tests' functions of `math` are `Exp` and `Log`; `requires` is empty; the nested module is `.devcontainer/gopls` (R14).
- **`ci-test`** is only the run with `-race` and coverage into `coverage/go.out`. "Go / arm64" calls it on `ubuntu-24.04-arm` and then its host's runs, so the former job "Go arm64" would repeat it.
- **The ruleset `main`** requires, instead of "Go arm64", four checks of `same-bits` — "Go / targets", "Go / fma-diff", "Go / arm64" and "Go / rules" — with `integration_id` 15368, after their green run on pull request #33 of T26; the other rules do not change. Without this the merge would wait for "Go arm64", which no longer exists. The author puts them: the classifier of Claude Code's auto mode forbade an edit of the ruleset from the executor's session as a bypass of CI, 2026-11-08.
- **Checked** (2026-11-08, in the container on amd64): `just ci-lint` and `just ci-test` green, `go mod tidy -diff` empty; the scenarios "Go / targets" and "Go / fma-diff" from `go.yml` v0.1.21 — green, `fma-diff` ok on six targets; the tool v0.1.21, built from a clone of `.github` in a temporary folder, on the module with the lists — 0 findings, and on `a*b + c` in the code and an import of `go/ast` in a test — two findings, `fused` and `import`, and exit code 1.

The lists are the repository's data, while the rules are one (R107 spec). The shared workflows are on one version, as R13 requires, and v0.1.21 is the latest release of `.github` (`gh release list -R altnovatrail/.github`, 2026-11-08).

— *Rejected:*
- v0.1.20: v0.1.21 has the same function, with the record of T48 and the archive of E14, and the move takes the latest release;
- keeping an own `rules_test.go` beside "Go / rules": two copies of the same rules, against which R107 spec decided;

- bypassing the release `v0.1.1` by not touching the `justfile` and `release.yml`: `ci-test` would call the removed recipes, and the shared workflows would stand at different versions.

## The gate of `sim`

**R20. The gate of `sim` is the job "Gate" in `ci.yml`: `sim` from git by the tag and the SHA of its commit, written in the job's `env`, with the tag checked by `git ls-remote`, and `sim`'s recipe `just gate` in the stage `toolchain` of `sim`'s devcontainer of the same tag, with the pull request's code in `GATE_LEARNER_MODEL`. It runs on every pull request and every commit of `main`, on `ubuntu-24.04`, with `timeout-minutes: 15`, without a Go cache, and "Gate" is a required check of the ruleset of `main`. The first tag is `sim` `v0.1.0`.** The author's decision of 2026-11-09 in planning T22 is to merge T26 and T27 first, and to check the red gate both in the container and in CI; the rest is the plan's proposal, which the author approved (RUN.md, T22).
- **What stands** (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`, job `gate`):
  - `env` — `SIM_TAG: v0.1.0` and `SIM_COMMIT: 09e5a84f8a26fedcf5c8996bf81bf29c616917a2`. The checkout of this repository is in `lm/`, `altnovatrail/sim` by `SIM_COMMIT` in `sim/`, both without credentials;
  - the step "sim tag" — the tag from `git ls-remote` must name `SIM_COMMIT`, an annotated one through `^{}`, or the job fails and names both. This is how `sim`'s recipe checks the tags of the libraries against `gate.json`;
  - the image — `docker build --target toolchain - < sim/.devcontainer/Dockerfile`;
  - the step "gate" — `docker run` on the same pattern as the job "Proto", with `--env GATE_LEARNER_MODEL=/work/lm` and `--workdir /work/sim`: `just gate`, the mode `bands`, and the table of the numbers against the bands goes to `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY`, with `pipefail`. The recipe writes `go.work` in a temporary folder — `use` of `sim`, of the pull request's code and of `engine` at the commit from `gate.json` —, does not commit it and does not touch the harness's `go.mod`. Code 0 means the gate is green, 1 that it is red or the input is rejected, 2 an unknown call;
  - the ruleset of `main` requires "Gate" with `integration_id` 15368, after its green run on the pull request of T22. The executor made the edit of the ruleset with the author's permission on 2026-11-09 — and the replacement of "Go arm64" by four checks `same-bits` by R18 too.
- **Checked** (2026-11-09):
  - in the container, on a clone of `sim` at `09e5a84` in the session's folder: `GATE_LEARNER_MODEL=/workspaces/learner-model just gate` — code 0 in 53 seconds, "The gate is green: every number is within their bands, and no learner breaks an invariant". With `bandStep = 3` instead of 2.5 in `model.go` — code 1, "The gate is red", and outside the bands, among others, `M1.rms.200` — 0.603 against the band 0.380 ± 2 × 0.020 and `M3.lag` — −0.726 against −1.242 ± 2 × 0.038. The edit was rolled back;
  - the step "sim tag" with the same text: with `v0.1.0` and `09e5a84` — 0, with another SHA and with a tag that does not exist — 1, and both are named;
  - CI is in RUN.md, T22, «Сделано».

— *Why:*
- **The tag and the SHA in `env`, not a reusable workflow of `sim`.** So R103 spec and `sim`'s README, "Taking a release", require, and `sim` deliberately gives no reusable workflow: the gate would half live in `sim` (R05 `sim`, "Rejected"). The SHA holds the code, the tag the reader and the check: a tag moved by mistake will not pass silently.
- **`sim`'s recipe, not a `go.work` of its own.** `just gate` already writes `go.work`, takes `engine` from `gate.json`, checks the build and the bands, and `GATE_LEARNER_MODEL` is its input for the caller (R26 `sim`). A recipe of its own would be a copy that would drift from it.
- **`sim`'s stage `toolchain`, not one of its own.** The gate checks the line `go` and the Go it is built with — R103 spec names `sim`'s devcontainer of the same tag, and then Go, just and git are the same as the harness's.
- **Only amd64, without a cache.** The same bits on amd64 and arm64 are held by `sim`'s CI with its `Gate / arm64`, and `ubuntu-24.04` is enough for the caller (3.9 `sim`). A Go cache, by the measurement of T24 `sim`, gives almost nothing, and the cache key is one more thing to keep.
- **On every pull request.** So R103 spec requires: an edit of a document will not move the gate, and to keep a list of the paths that call it would mean guessing what the model reads. Four minutes of a public repository's runner cost nothing (R30 spec).

— *Rejected:*
- a reusable `gate.yml` in `sim` or in `.github` — `sim` rejected it (R05 `sim`), and R103 spec names the job the caller's own;
- `go mod edit -replace` instead of `go.work` — it edits the harness's `go.mod`, rejected by R103 spec;
- a matrix of amd64 and arm64, as in `sim`'s CI — a repeat of what `sim` already holds;
- a Go cache on `sim`'s pattern;
- the gate only on commits that change the code — paths outside the release's `paths` pass the gate anyway, and the rule "on every pull request" is R103 spec.

— *Consequence:* `.github/workflows/ci.yml`; the ruleset of `main`; the 2026-11-09 amendments to R12 and R15; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1, 3.6 and 4; README.md, CLAUDE.md; RUN.md, T22 and row В30. A candidate that moves the numbers goes by the order of releases of R103 spec: first the bands in `sim` and its new tag, then the raise of `SIM_TAG` and `SIM_COMMIT` here. `ci.yml` is not in the release's `paths`, so the merge of T22 gives no release.

## The leading question of format 0.4

**R21. The leading question of a task of format 0.4 is help, as a technique on request: the answer after it goes into the step, the counters and the stability as it is, but does not set "mastered". The evidence carries it by a field of its own, apart from `technique_on_request`, and this is a fact of the attempt. The code comes with format 0.4.** The author's answer of 2026-11-09 to В26 (RUN.md, T27), by option 1 — the recommendation of [architecture.md](architecture.md), subsection 2.8.
- **What is decided:** the evidence has an optional field "the learner got a leading question", beside the technique on request; `api` gives the question for a task, not for an item, before the first wrong answer (the 2026-11-08 amendment to R82 spec). If it is present, every answer of the attempt is with help: the same rule as for a technique on request (R06; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.3). There is no new number. The field's name and its number in the schema are given by the code's task;
- **when:** with the move to a tag of `spec` of the line 0.10, where format 0.4 will come out, together with the value `time` of `CheckKind` (R19) — a change after the baseline, through the gate of `sim` (R12, R20): debt Д14 in table 4 of RUN.md. The field comes out in a release of `learner-model` before `api` starts writing it, and `api` writes the question only with it, not with `technique_on_request` (row В27).

— *Why:* the question hints at the method more strongly than a technique — that is why the format closed it (R57 spec; R108 spec, В31) —, so it weighs no less than a technique, and help is the strongest that R06 gives without a new number. A field of its own keeps the fact apart: the log is append-only, and if the harness shows that the question must weigh otherwise than a technique, a new version will read it by recomputation (R08).

— *Rejected:*
- the same rule by the field `technique_on_request` — the log would mix the two facts forever, and splitting them by recomputation would no longer be possible;
- nothing, as a technique at the step — the answer after a question that hinted at the method would set "mastered";
- a smaller weight of the answer — one more constant without data, which R06 rejected.

— *Consequence:* [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 2, 2.8 and 3.3; README.md, CLAUDE.md; RUN.md — row В26 left table 3, Д14 and row В27 name the decision. The code and the schema come with the task of the move to a tag of the line 0.10.

— *Amendment 2026-11-09:* **"when" and "The code and the schema come with the task of the move to a tag of the line 0.10" above read so: by a task of its own after the move, through the gate of `sim`, in one pull request and one release.** The move came as task T34 to `spec` v0.12.0, without code (R23): format 0.4 is already there, and the task has nothing more to wait for. The weight, the field and the order — the release before `api` starts writing the field — are the same, and `spec` recorded them by the 2026-11-09 amendment to R82 spec, by row В27. — R23; RUN.md, T34 and Д14.

— *Amendment 2026-11-11:* **the field is `leading_question_on_request = 20` of the evidence of kind 2, in Go `Evidence.LeadingQuestionOnRequest`; it came with task T37 (R28).** The weight is the same: help, as a technique on request, through the rule `helped`. — R28; RUN.md, T37.

## The plan of E3

**R22. Epic E3 is the first candidates of the model through the gate of `sim`. Up to three candidates on the numbers Д5–Д7 go into `sim`'s first judgement, each by its own draft pull request, and an exploration chooses them — rough runs of the harness in the container. The numbers that `engine` shares — the threshold "mastered" and the boundary of failure ½ — do not go into the first judgement. The test vectors of standalone that pin an old number are removed by the candidate, with a record.** The author's answers of 2026-11-09 in planning E3, all four by the plan's recommendations (RUN.md, table 1).
- **What was found in planning** (`sim` on `main` a1bf796, read 2026-11-09):
  - `sim`'s E3 is split into T29–T35. The judgement of a candidate of the model is T32 `sim`, written for one candidate, though the file of the judgement names the candidates as a list (R25 `sim`) — how many it judges at a time is asked by row В31. It waits for a new pin and tag of `sim` — T30, `modules` v0.1.4 and new paths —, for the deciding run on the candidate's commit through `go.work` — T31 — and for the candidate's pull request here. `sim` will not judge candidates on `v0.1.0`, and the gates of the libraries move to the tag of T30 (В9 RUN.md `sim`);
  - the judgement is a deciding run, 4,000 learners per cell, about 35 minutes in the container on 20 cores, then `sim judge` by the model's criterion: the constraints "no worse" and the estimate of the groups. A candidate names its group and its target, the number of changed constants and a new field, if there is one (R25 `sim`). Of those that passed the simplest is chosen, and the chosen one is confirmed on held-out learners — otherwise the exit is to the base (R04 `sim`);
  - the numbers Д5–Д7 are named constants of `model.go`, but for ρ, the boundary ½, the halving of τ and the guessing of `predicate`, which stand in formulas, and C, hard-wired in `detmath` and in `reviewDate`. There is no constant of the model's version in the code: the cache's mark is the release's tag (R80 spec).
- **What is decided:**
  1. up to three candidates go into the first judgement, each by its own draft pull request: one constant or a coherent set, with its own group of the estimate. If `sim` judges one at a time (В31), they go in turn, the best by the exploration first;
  2. the candidates are chosen by an exploration — rough runs of `sim` on the numbers Д5–Д7, one at a time, on fewer learners than a deciding run. They decide nothing but show which number moves which measure; the result is an entry of the log with the candidates and the rejected ones. The constants step both ways, and the edges — guessing 0 and full weight — only inwards. The rule of τ by the share of correct answers and C do not go into the exploration: the first is a rule, not a number, and C is hard-wired into a formula and into an exported constant of `detmath`, and changing it is the model's design and the API of `detmath` (the plan, the executor's proposal after review);
  3. the threshold "mastered" only in a pair with the target of `engine`'s corridor, and the boundary ½ is read by `engine` too, with R107 spec: neither is in the first judgement, Д16 and Д7;
  4. the vectors of standalone that pin an old number are removed by the candidate, with a record in the log. The baseline with them is held by the tag `v0.1.0`, and the new one by the gate and the golden vectors.
- **The tasks** are T29–T33 of RUN.md: the gate on the tag of T30 `sim`, the exploration, the candidates, the judgement and the release, the result.

— *Why:* of those that passed, `sim`'s criterion chooses the simplest, so several candidates in one judgement give more chances than one, and a deciding run for each takes about 35 minutes. An exploration is cheaper than a judgement, and without it the judgement would more likely reject all. A pair with `engine` needs its candidate and a paired criterion, and R103 spec does not name how to release the boundary ½ together with `engine` (R17); the first judgement does not wait for it. The vectors of standalone are printed for standalone's numbers: a candidate that changes a number is not supposed to pass them, and keeping them on parameters would mean testing code that does not go into the release.

— *Rejected:*
- one candidate — if it does not pass, one more round of judgement is needed;
- candidates by reasoning, without runs — faster, but blind;
- the pair of the threshold and the target of the corridor in the first judgement — it needs synchronous work with `engine`'s session and its candidate;
- the model's constants as an internal set, so that the vectors of standalone run on its numbers — more code, and a separate task would have to go first.

— *Consequence:* RUN.md — E3 in table 1 with T29–T33, the row of E3 left table 2, Д16, «Когда вернуться» of Д5–Д7 and row В31; CLAUDE.md, README.md and [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 1 — E3 is under way.

— *Amendment 2026-11-10:* **the "about 35 minutes" of a deciding run above is the time of the old pin: on `olympiad-math` 0.1.1 the run of the base takes 6 h 43 min on 20 cores, and that of the engine's ceiling 1 h 31 min (RUN.md `sim`, T30). This does not change decisions 1–4: there is one judgement per round of candidates (the 2026-11-09 amendment to R103 spec), and an exploration is all the cheaper than it.** Fitting the run into an hour is T36 `sim`, before the deciding run of the candidates (T31 `sim`). — R24; RUN.md, table 1.

## The exploration of E3

**R24. Three candidates go into `sim`'s first judgement, all in the group `forgets`, without a target: g = 10 — one constant; the base of the division of τ 0.35 instead of 0.5 — one; and their coherent set — two. They were chosen by the exploration of T30 — rough runs of the harness on 15 variants of the numbers Д5–Д7, one at a time, at 100 learners per cell, and then of the four best and the coherent set at 300. The other numbers are rejected with what the exploration showed, and the harness does not measure the guessing of `predicate`.** The author's decisions of 2026-11-10 in planning T30 — 100 learners, then 300 for the five best, and the steps by the plan's table; the choice by the results of the exploration, the executor's proposal (RUN.md, T30).
- **The harness** — `sim` `v0.1.2`, commit e61f90a, the tag the gate stands on (the 2026-11-09 amendment to R20): `modules` v0.1.4 with `olympiad-math` 0.1.1, `engine` `v0.1.0` from `sim`'s `go.mod` (В33), the set `working`, the rule `engine`, all populations. `sim` has no recipe yet for a run of another `learner-model` (T31 `sim`), so the run is put together as the gate's recipe puts it: `go.work` in the session's folder with a clone of `sim` and a copy of `learner-model` at the variant's commit, 20 parts `sim run -learners N -part k/20 -commit github.com/altnovatrail/learner-model=<sha>` and `sim merge`. The base is the code of `main` 4f3cfbb. The variants are a commit each on a branch of the clone in the session's folder; neither they nor the files of the runs go into the repository. The SHA-256 of the base: at 100 learners `700bf41b03673fad…20e`, at 300 `6dc6a481c32d8182…be8`; a repeat of the variant "base 0.35" at 100 learners gave the same file, `b70827557e181bff…b69`, to the byte.
- **The analysis** — `sim judge` of the rough look, "a rough look, which decides nothing": a variant stands in the file of the judgement as a candidate for each of the eight groups of the model's criterion and for each target, and `sim compare` gives the differences by measure. The overall score is read only when the candidate's run reads every number with a path (`judge.go`, `score`), and at 100 learners it is not read for any variant, nor for the base against itself: stage 1 ranks by the score of the groups and by the constraints, and the overall score is at 300.
- **Stage 1**, 100 learners per cell — the score of the groups (↑ and ↓ — the interval is wholly above or below zero) and the number of violated constraints "no worse" and of the floor:

  | Variant | `still` | `learns` | `jumps` | `off-band` | `forgets` | `pages` | Violated |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | `bandStep` 2.0 | −0.138↓ | −0.234↓ | — | −0.184↓ | +0.141 | 0 | 91 |
  | `bandStep` 3.0 | −0.156↓ | +0.219↑ | +0.121↑ | −0.003 | −0.543↓ | 0 | 83 |
  | σ₀ 2.0 | −0.001 | +0.015 | −0.058 | −0.217↓ | +0.205 | +0.018↑ | 12 |
  | σ₀ 3.0 | −0.004 | +0.020↑ | −0.007 | +0.060↑ | +0.001 | −0.027↓ | 10 |
  | I 0.12 | +0.002 | −0.001 | −0.010 | 0 | +0.205 | +0.002 | 10 |
  | I 0.202148 | −0.001 | +0.005 | −0.005 | 0 | −0.117 | −0.009 | 9 |
  | τ₀ 0.5 days | −0.019↓ | +0.011 | −0.011 | 0 | +0.121 | −0.016↓ | 26 |
  | τ₀ 2 days | −0.005 | −0.018↓ | +0.012 | 0 | −0.313↓ | −0.007↓ | 44 |
  | g 10 | +0.002 | +0.011↑ | +0.015 | 0 | +0.372 | 0 | 3 |
  | g 22.5 | −0.006 | 0 | +0.017 | 0 | +0.051 | 0 | 10 |
  | base 0.35 | −0.002 | +0.002 | +0.025 | 0 | +0.517↑ | 0 | 2 |
  | base 0.7 | 0 | −0.006 | +0.036 | 0 | +0.099 | 0 | 14 |
  | ρ 1/4 | −0.019 | +0.024↑ | +0.041↑ | +0.020↑ | −0.070 | +0.005 | 5 |
  | ρ 1/2 | +0.009 | −0.029↓ | −0.034 | −0.036↓ | +0.372↑ | −0.001 | 20 |
  | help and repeat of a task 1/2 | −0.022 | −0.086↓ | −0.045 | 0 | +0.289 | −0.061↓ | 39 |

  "—" — the group's score is not read. The groups `mastered` and `anchor` are not read at all at 100 learners. 100 learners is below the edge of `sim`'s rough look, 300–1,000 (3.6 `sim`): stage 1 chooses nothing by itself but only sifts for stage 2 — by the sign of the groups and the number of violations. A variant's run took from 10 to 45 minutes on 20 cores — longer when the host was busy from outside.
- **Stage 2**, 300 learners per cell — five runs, as decision 1 requires, "up to the five best": the four best variants of stage 1 and the coherent set of the two numbers that move `forgets` in the same direction. The best are those that have at least one group improved beyond the noise and no more than ten constraints violated. Not selected: ρ 1/2 — `forgets` +0.372↑, but `learns` and `off-band` ↓ and 20 violations; I 0.202148 — 9 violations, but no group moved beyond the noise, and `forgets` −0.117:

  | Variant | Overall score | `forgets` | Other groups | Violated |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | g 10 and base 0.35 | +0.060 [0.023; 0.103] | +0.457 [0.183; 0.784] | within the noise | 8: the corridor `E1` on six populations of the olympiad one, `M1` on G3-drop, `M10` on F-none of the school one, `M3.settle` on F-break — all barely past the tolerance |
  | g 10 | +0.052 [0.018; 0.093] | +0.396 [0.148; 0.702] | within the noise | 4: `E1` on G2, `M1` on G3-drop, `M5.caught` and `M10` on F-none of the school one — all barely past the tolerance |
  | base 0.35 | +0.039 [0.006; 0.079] | +0.315 [0.060; 0.636] | within the noise | 1: `M10.retention` on F-none of the school one, 0.00245 at the tolerance 0.00202 |
  | σ₀ 3.0 | +0.006 [−0.026; 0.042] | +0.089 | `learns` +0.029↑, `off-band` +0.062↑, `still` −0.015↓, `pages` −0.020↓ | 28 |
  | ρ 1/4 | −0.038 [−0.098; 0.020] | −0.283↓ | `learns` +0.026↑, `jumps` +0.020↑, `off-band` +0.020↑ | 18 and the floor `M4` on G0 of the school one: 0.26 at the floor 0.20 |

  No target — `lag`, `corridor`, `settle` — is reached by any variant; the base is far from them: on G3 the share of those "not settled" is 0.88 for the olympiad one and 0.59 for the school one, at the target 0.25.
- **What is decided:**
  1. three candidates go into the judgement — `g10`: `stabilityGrowth` 10 instead of 15; `halve035`: the base of the division of τ 0.35 instead of 0.5; `forget-set`: both. The group of all of them is `forgets`, there is no target, no new field; constants — 1, 1 and 2. Of those that passed `sim` chooses the simplest (R04 `sim`), so the set wins only if its score is above the single ones' beyond the noise;
  2. rejected: `bandStep` — both ways it breaks the error of the estimate `M1` and the corridor `E1` on olympiad maths — dozens of constraints —, and 3.0 besides worsens `forgets`; σ₀ — 2.0 worsens `off-band`, and 3.0 at 300 learners is no better than the base and violates 28 constraints; I — in neither direction moves groups beyond the noise; τ₀ — worse both ways, and 2 days worsens `forgets` too; g 22.5 and base 0.7 — within the noise; ρ — 1/4 worsens `forgets` and breaks the floor, and 1/2 improves `forgets` at the cost of `learns` and `off-band`; the weight 1/2 of an answer with help — worse `learns` and `pages`. The variant took the weight of a repeat of a task too: `helped` counts as help also the solution of this task that has already gone to the device ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.3), so there is no separate variant of a repeat;
  3. the guessing of `predicate` did not go into the exploration: no pinned package has a check `predicate` — `grade-3-math` 0.1.0 and `olympiad-math` 0.1.1 have `choice`, `number`, `fraction` and sheets, `english-essays` 0.1.0 has `choice`, `rubric` and sheets (the packages' `server.json`) —, and the synthetic `S-predicate` runs as a cell "without a graph", whose checks are "neither met nor failed". The number stays in Д5 until a module with `predicate`.

— *Why so:* both chosen numbers are constants of forgetting of R04, taken from systems for flashcards, and the exploration showed that the model catches the forgetting of a skill better when the stability grows more slowly and a wrong answer cuts it more (Д6). Both numbers hardly touch the other groups, and the constraints they violate are on the edge of the tolerance, and the base 0.35 has only one. The set — because both numbers move one group in one direction, and the judgement must see whether they add up.

— *What the exploration does not decide:* a rough look decides nothing (3.6 `sim`). At 300 learners no variant holds all the constraints "no worse", and a deciding run at 4,000 learners may not hold them either: its resolution `4,3·SE` is narrower, and the author's tolerance is the same. The exploration ran on `engine` `v0.1.0`; if `sim` raises `engine` to `v0.1.3` by В33, the judgement will run on a new base, and the directions will probably stay: the edit of `engine` is a lesson for a skill from the map, not forgetting.

— *Rejected:*
- a candidate for each group — for σ₀ 3.0 and ρ 1/4 the best groups are paid for by others and by broken constraints, and the criterion judges the overall score;
- more than three candidates — R22;
- a run at 1,000 learners for the three best — about 1 h 40 min per variant on a free machine and more on a busy one, and it does not change the choice: the intervals of the overall score of the three are already above zero.

— *Consequence:* T31 writes three draft pull requests; the base of the division of τ, which stands in the formula `stabilityAfter`, the candidate makes a named constant. The rows Д5–Д7 in table 4 of RUN.md — what the exploration learned. — RUN.md, T30, T31, Д5–Д7; R04, R22; the 2026-11-09 amendment to R20; В33.

— *Amendment 2026-11-10:* **`sim`'s first judgement of the three candidates ended with an exit: all three are better than the base by the estimate, but none holds all the constraints "no worse", and the base stays — g = 15 and the halving of τ. The draft pull requests #43–45 are closed with a reason, and the numbers stay in Д6.** The judgement is T32 of RUN.md `sim`, R37 `sim`, the report is `sim`'s `judgements/model-1/` (`main` 0364b40, read 2026-11-10): deciding runs at 1,000 working learners per cell — that is what a deciding run weighs since T36 `sim` —, on `engine` and `learner-model` v0.1.4 of the base and on the candidates' commits, 41–44 minutes each.

  | Candidate | Estimate | `forgets` | Failed "no worse" |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | `g10` | 0.025 [0.011; 0.037] | 0.195 [0.098; 0.294] | 4: `M1.rms.200` on G3-drop, G8 and S-scale1.5 of the olympiad one and `M3.settle` on F-break |
  | `halve035` | 0.014 [0.002; 0.027] | 0.117 [0.031; 0.213] | 3: on the edge of the tolerance — `E3.late` on F-growth5, 0.04967 at 0.049, and `M1.rms.200` on A-unpractised, 0.03116 at 0.03076 —, and `M3.settle` on F-break — 19.42 at 18.11, 7 % past the tolerance |
  | `forget-set` | 0.033 [0.020; 0.046] | 0.277 [0.184; 0.373] | 8: the same as `g10`'s, `E1.corridor` on G2, G2-fading and S-adult and `M1.rms.200` on F-none of the olympiad one |

  What the judgement showed beyond the exploration:
  - the gain in `forgets` was confirmed, but 1.7–2.7 times smaller than at the rough look: at 300 learners — 0.40, 0.32 and 0.46;
  - the common failure of the three is the catching up after a break, `M3.settle` on F-break: with the new numbers the model catches up with the truth after a break for longer. The exploration saw it only for the set, and for `g10`, of `M1.rms.200` on the olympiad one, only G3-drop, without G8 and S-scale1.5: at 300 learners the resolution of "no worse" is wider, and these failures passed for noise. Why the catching up is slower, the judgement does not read: that is a question of the next round, together with Д5–Д7;
  - there was no confirmation, and `held-out-model-1` is still deferred (R37 `sim`).

  — *Why an exit, not a tolerance:* the tolerances "no worse" were recorded before the first candidate, and they may not be shifted for a candidate (R04 `sim`; R163 standalone). — *Consequence:* nothing changes in the code, tests and documents of `main`: the amendments to R04 and the edits of sections 3.2 and 3.3 lived only in the drafts. RUN.md — T32, Д6, row В35 «в реестр spec» to row 4.9 of `COORDINATION.md` and debt Д17 — the gate on the former tag of `sim`. — RUN.md, T32; R37 `sim`.

— *Amendment 2026-11-16 (R40):* decision 2's rejection of σ₀ was by the overall score, before `sim` measured the fairness by the grade. That measurement is the new fact: the base does not hold its criterion (R58 sim). σ₀, the trial series and the start by the grade come back as the candidates of E8, measured by `M11` and judged by `sim`'s E10 (R40); `off-band` +0.062 of σ₀ 3.0 above is a lead for the exploration T61.

— *Amendment 2026-11-16 (T59; R41):* on the cells `D-` σ₀ 3.0 brings `M11.same` of grade 1 to 0.9135 and leaves grade 5 at 0.5725, and σ₀ 2.0 and 4.0 are worse under both grades: under grade 5 the tasks of the trial series move with the start, and a prior of any width moves with them. σ₀ goes into T61 only together with the step after the series and the weight of the series (R41, decision 2).

— *Amendment 2026-11-16 (T61; R42):* at the size of the deciding run, σ₀ 3.0 alone fails 17 checks "no worse" on twelve cells of risk alone — the error of the estimate after the 200th answer and Brier on both maths, the start of `S-adult` —, as decision 2 found at 300 learners. With the step after the series no set holds every constraint either, and the round's long runs stop by the author's decision of 2026-11-16 (R42).

## The plan of E4

**R25. Epic E4 is format 0.4 in the model. First the move to the latest tag of `spec` and the gate on the latest tag of `sim`, then the value `time` of `CheckKind` and the field of the leading question in one pull request through the gate and in one release, then the result. The executor takes every task of the epic but T37 through to the merge into `main` himself, and T37 the author reads through before the merge.** The author's answer of 2026-11-11 to В36 — take E4, by the row's recommendation —, and the plan of the unfolding, which the author approved the same day (RUN.md, table 1).
- **What was found in planning** (`gh release list`, clones of `spec` and `sim` on `main`, read 2026-11-11):
  - the latest release of `spec` is v0.14.0, and `main` stands on it. Row 4.10 of `COORDINATION.md` sets the order: a release of `learner-model` with `time` and the field of the leading question, then `sim` reads them and releases a tag, and only then `modules` releases v0.2.1; `api` takes the same release (row 6.5; Д138 and Д173 in table 4 of RUN.md `spec`). The tag also brings `learner-model` Д181: `api` puts the `.proto` of the version that computed into the export, so the comments of the `.proto` must explain each field without code, and the release must name the `.proto` file a file of the export;
  - the latest tag of `sim` is v0.1.5, commit `dd0391958105195cb21193db1c2b9fbc71b2fe1e`: `engine` and `learner-model` v0.1.4, `modules` v0.2.0 of format 0.4, where there is neither `time` nor leading questions. So the code of Д14 does not move the gate's numbers. `sim`'s E4 «Стенд за библиотеками» is under way: its T39 will release a tag on `engine` v0.1.5, and T42 waits for the release of Д14 from here (RUN.md `sim`, `main` d001515);
  - the code of Д14 is the value `CHECK_KIND_TIME` in `common.proto`, the field in `evidence.proto` after `technique_on_request`, the types, the codec, the check kind in `knownChecks` (`step.go`), which the step, the trial series and `Readout.Task` call, and the rule `helped`. `time` already has no guessing: only `choice` gives it (`model.go`). Whether to raise `EvidenceKind` to 2 — R08 changes the kind when a field is added — is decided in planning the task of the code.
- **What is decided:**
  1. E4 is the next epic, and the second round of candidates, E5, comes after it;
  2. the tasks are T35 «Переход на `spec` v0.14.0», T36 «Гейт на последнем теге `sim`» (Д17), T37 «`time` и наводящий вопрос — одним релизом» (Д14) and T38 «Итог эпика», in this order. The gate takes the tag of `sim` that is the latest at the time of T36; if `sim` releases a new one before the merge of T37, the gate is raised to it by a repeat of T36 — by its own pull request on the code of `main` —, so that a red gate does not confuse new bands with new code. Д181 goes into T37: it edits the `.proto`, and the schema comes out with a release;
  3. the executor himself commits, opens the pull request, waits for green CI and merges each task of E4 into `main` — a departure from rule 7 of RUN.md and from "Do not commit" of RULES.md, as in T29–T34. Except T37: he takes it to green CI and "Gate" and waits, and merges after the author has read the pull request through — the merge releases an immutable release with the schema that `sim` and `api` take, and `api` puts its `.proto` into the export (Д181), so an edit after the merge would become a new release and a recomputation of the cache in `api`. This is the author's answer to a finding of the plan's review.

— *Why so:* row 4.10 puts this release before `sim` and `modules` v0.2.1, and T42 `sim` and `api` wait for it (Д138). A second round without an answer to why the model takes longer to catch up with the truth after a break would repeat the first (the 2026-11-10 amendment to R24). The move goes first, because row 4.10 and Д181 come with the tag, and the repository moves to a new tag by a task of its own (RULES.md, Across repositories). The gate goes before the code, as T29 in E3: the code must pass the gate on the pin on which `sim` will read `time`.

— *Rejected:*

- the code before the move and the gate — the release would pass the gate on the bands of `sim` v0.1.2 with `engine` v0.1.0, not on the pin where `sim` will read it (Д17);
- the move and the gate as one task — each has its own check and its own risk: the move releases a patch with the same code (the 2026-11-09 amendment to R16), and the gate may turn red through no fault of the code.

— *Consequence:* RUN.md — E4 in table 1 with T35–T38, the row of E4 left table 2, В36 is closed, Д14 and Д17 have «Когда вернуться»; CLAUDE.md and README.md — E4 is under way.

— *Amendment 2026-11-11:* **E4 has a fifth task, T40 «`Readout.Task` быстрее» (Д18), after T37 and before T38, and the executor merges T37 himself too.** The author's answers of 2026-11-11: to В37 — «а», by a task of its own in E4 after T37, by the row's recommendation (R27); and in the execution of T37 — «сам доведёшь изменения до мержа в main», so decision 3 no longer holds T37 for the author's reading. T38 waits for T40 too. — RUN.md, table 1, T37, T38, T40, В37 and Д18.

## Format 0.4 in the model

**R28. Format 0.4 in the model's code: the value `CHECK_KIND_TIME = 5` of `CheckKind` with guessing 0 and the field `leading_question_on_request = 20` of the evidence — help, as a technique on request. With the field came the evidence kind 2: it always writes the field, and in kind 1 the field is "not recorded" and is read as "there was no question". The state kind stays 1, the edit does not change the model's numbers, and the comments of the `.proto` are written for the reader of the export (Д181 RUN.md `spec`). In one pull request through the gate of `sim` and in one release.** The author's answers of 2026-11-11 in planning T37 — the name `leading_question_on_request` and kind 2, both by the plan's recommendations; the rest is the plan, which the author approved (RUN.md, T37).
- **What was found in planning** (the code of `main` beafeaa; `sim` and `engine` v0.1.6, read 2026-11-11):
  - the model would read `time` as an item without an answer in one place — `knownChecks` (`step.go`), where the boundary was `CheckKindPredicate`. It is called by the item's answer, the check of an answer of the trial series in the state and `Readout.Task`, so one edit gives `time` to all three. Guessing is already 0 for everything that is not `choice` (`guess`, `model.go`); the codec writes and reads any value of `CheckKind`; there is no `switch` on it anywhere;
  - an attempt where all items are `time` did not move the module and lost its traps: `move` exits before `countTraps`. Now the traps are counted — for example `twelve-hours-off` of the format's example (the 2026-11-10 amendment to R57 spec);
  - the field 15 `technique_on_request` is always written and required in every kind, and in the logic only `helped` reads it; the new field follows the same pattern, but number 20 is written last, after the traps (19);
  - the edit does not break `sim` and `engine` v0.1.6: their literals of `Evidence`, `Task` and `Slot` are by field names, and the translation of a package by `sim` refuses `time` with its own map until T42 `sim` takes it. `sim` compares the bytes of evidence only between two runs, so the gate's numbers do not move: the packages of `modules` v0.2.0 have neither `time` nor leading questions.
- **What is decided:**
  1. **the value `CHECK_KIND_TIME = 5`**, in Go `CheckKindTime`, is a free answer without guessing (R19). It is read the same in every evidence kind: a new value is already an addition that R08 allows, and nobody wrote kind 1 with `time` — the value was not in the schema;
  2. **the field `leading_question_on_request = 20`**, in Go `Evidence.LeadingQuestionOnRequest`: whether the button "Hint" gave the task's leading question. If it did, every answer of the attempt is with help, through `helped`, as with a technique on request (R21). The name follows the neighbouring `technique_on_request`, the number is the next free one;
  3. **the evidence kind 2**, `EvidenceKind = 2`. The kind changes when a field is added, and in the former kind the field is "not recorded", not "no" (R08; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.5): `api`'s log holds records of kind 1, where the question could not be recorded, and they must stay distinguishable from records where it was not given. The writer writes field 20 only in kind 2 and newer, and always, `false` too; the strict reader requires it there and rejects kind 1 with field 20; `EncodeEvidence` and `Append` reject kind 1 where the field is set. So the bytes of kind 1 from the log after reading and writing are the same bytes, and "not recorded" is read as "there was no question" — as the former version read kind 1: before format 0.4 there were no leading questions. The state kind is 1: the state got no new fields;
  4. **the comments of the `.proto` are for the reader of the export** (Д181 RUN.md `spec`; R135 spec): `api` puts the `common.proto`, `evidence.proto` and `state.proto` of the version that computed beside the JSON of the log and the state, in `api/proto/novatrail/learnermodel/v1/`. All comments were checked: the headers name the repository and the export, the fields of help say that an answer with them does not set "mastered", the field of the technique at the step that the model does not read it, the level θ the scale of the bands, the kind that kind 2 was added. That the archive's files are the ones `api` puts into the export is said by the README, "Releases", and by [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.7: the release's text is written by the shared `release.yml` of `.github`.
- **Checked** (2026-11-11): `just ci-test` — coverage 98.2 %; `just ci-lint`; `just ci-proto` — `buf breaking` against `v0.1.4` is green, the codec and the schema agree both ways; `just ci-release`. The golden logs `testdata/fold/` of the five former logs — bytes of kind 1, as `v0.1.4` wrote them — did not change by a byte and give the same states, and the new `format04` shows that a page after a leading question moves the skill just as without it, but only the page without the question masters it. `just gate exact` from a clone of `sim` `v0.1.8` in the container on this code is green, all 69 numbers to the bit, 98 seconds.

— *Why so:* format 0.4 is out, `sim` and `modules` wait for this release by row 4.10 of `spec`'s `COORDINATION.md`, and `api` writes the field only with it (Д138, Д173 RUN.md `spec`). Kind 2 keeps the distinction that R08 recorded as a rule, and writing the field only in kind 2 is what keeps the codec's promise: what the reader accepted, the writer gives back in the same bytes.

— *Rejected:*
- kind 1 and the field only when `true` — less code, but "the question was not given" and "`api` did not record it" could no longer be told apart in the log, and R08 requires keeping exactly this distinction;
- field 20 always, in kind 1 too — the bytes of kind 1 from the log after reading and writing would become different, and the codec's promise would break;
- `requested_leading_question`, by `spec`'s contract — there it is a string with the text of the question in the conversation between `app` and `api`, and here it is a fact of the attempt beside `technique_on_request`;
- `CHECK_KIND_TIME` only in kind 2 — a needless condition in the step, and there is nothing to distinguish: kind 1 with this value does not occur.

— *Consequence:* the merge changes the release's `paths` (R16) and releases `v0.1.5`. `api`'s cache is marked with the model's version (R80 spec), so `api`, taking it, will recompute the states, but the edit does not change the numbers of the former logs. `spec`, `sim` and `api` learn of the release by a row «в реестр spec»; Д14 is closed. — R08, R10, R16, R19, R21; the 2026-11-11 amendments to R19 and R21; RUN.md, T37 and Д14; README.md; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 2.8, 3.1, 3.3, 3.5 and 3.7; `proto/`, `types.go`, `evidence.go`, `codec.go`, `codec_read.go`, `codec_check.go`, `step.go` and the tests.

**R29. `Readout.Task` is faster with the same P to the bit and the same refusals: an ordinary call copies nothing but the slice of the answer. Tags and items that are already in order are read as they are, the module is a substring of the ID, `transfer` gives the graph's skill without a copy, the shares of the transfer are gathered only by the step, and the retentions of the skills at t₀ and the starting point of a module without a state are computed once by `Read`. The API is the same; the reference is `Task` of version `v0.1.5` in the tests.** The author's answers of 2026-11-11 in the planning of T40 — to speed it up inside, with the same API, and to measure `engine.Choose` too — both by the plan's recommendations; the rest is the plan the author approved (RUN.md, T40).
- **What was found in the planning and the measurement** (the code of `main` 355affc; `engine` `main` d0bb4d5; 2026-11-11):
  - in Д198 of `spec`'s RUN.md, 1.34–1.43 ms is the time of the whole choice, not of a call: a third of it goes to 772 calls — about 0.6 µs per call. That is not the computation but the work around it: the profile of `go test -bench` with `-cpuprofile` gave `Task` 79% of the benchmark's time, and in it a notable share is memory allocation and garbage collection, while `detmath` is about 3.5%. Allocations: the copy with sorting of the tags in `idList` and `chanceOf` — 23%, `[]Skill{s}` in `transfer` — 12%, insertions of retentions into `rs` — 11% and of shares into `shares`, which `Task` does not read, — 11%, the copy of the items — 11%, `strings.SplitN` in `moduleOf` — 11%; and the concatenation `"task "+t.ID` before the check;
  - the package `olympiad-math` 0.1.1 from `engine`'s data: three bands, 19 skills, all with a verdict, 772 tasks of one item and one primary skill each, 32 of them with one supporting skill; answer slots — 569 `number`, 196 `choice` of five, 7 `fraction`; 19 templates. The package's tags in a task are in the module's order, so the path with a copy remains for tags out of order.
- **What was decided:**
  1. **checks without copies.** `idList` sorts a copy only if the tags are out of order (`sortedView`, beside `sortedCopy` in `graph.go`); `Task` checks the tags of the task and the items with the function `checkTaskTags` — which is `checkTaskContent` without the items' places — and checks the places on the items in order, with the same message; it copies and sorts the items only if they are not in place. `checkTaskPoint` concatenates the name only on a refusal. `moduleOf` finds the three `/` with the function `strings.IndexByte` and gives the module as a substring of the ID, with the same refusals as `SplitN`. The order of the checks and their messages are the same;
  2. **transfer without copies.** `transfer` gives the graph's skill as a slice of the graph itself, `g.Skills[i:i+1:i+1]` — it is only read — and `chanceOf` takes the item's skills without a copy if they are in order, and gathers the shares of the transfer into a slice the step gives it, and at reading into none. σ, R̄ and P are still computed by one function for the step and for reading ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.7);
  3. **retentions at t₀ are in `Read`.** For each module whose current graph `Read` checked, it gives once the R at t₀ of each skill of the graph with a verdict, by ID, with the step's function `retention` — the R of the skill's state, or 1 without one — and the starting point of a module without a state from its own reading. `Task` only reads them: the transfer gives only skills of the graph with a verdict, so each R is found and none is inserted, and the slice is cut by `slices.Clip`, so that an insertion, were there one, would copy it. After `Read` the `Readout` does not change, and `Task` can be called from many calls at once (section 3.6, rule 7). A candidate of a module that `Read` did not read goes the old path;
  4. **the reference is `Task` of version `v0.1.5`.** `task_reference_internal_test.go` holds its text and the texts of its checks word for word, but for the names and two lines: which modules `Read` checked, the reference learns from a new field, and it takes P from `chanceOf` the way the step calls it — R anew, into an empty list, and with the shares of the transfer: this is the very computation `v0.1.5` did at reading. The reference has no copy of its own of `chanceOf` and `transfer` — SonarCloud took it for duplicated code, 4.5% against a threshold of 3% — so the reference holds `Task`'s checks, its R and the starting point, which `Read` now computes, to `v0.1.5`, and P to the step. `TestTaskIsReference` compares `Task` with it to the bit and a refusal to the word: on each prefix of the golden logs in three readings of `readGolden`, on the random logs of the property and on the benchmark's pool, with each task of the log and its variants — tags and items in another order, a repeated tag and place, a choice of one option, options without a mark, place 0, difficulty 6, an ID without a module, not UTF-8, a module with no entry in the graphs and a module that `Read` did not read. `TestModuleOfIsReference` compares `moduleOf` with the former `SplitN` on IDs of every form. The test goes red if the time of the retentions is moved by a second or a word in a refusal is changed. `TestTaskAllocatesOnce` holds one allocation per call on the benchmark's pool: so it shows if the transfer gives a skill whose R `Read` did not compute — then P is the same, but the slice is copied; `TestTaskConcurrently` calls `Task` of one `Readout` from four goroutines at once, under `-race` in `ci-test`;
  5. **the benchmark** is `BenchmarkReadoutTask` in `bench_test.go`: a synthetic module of the shape of `olympiad-math` 0.1.1 and four states, as in `engine`'s benchmark: a new learner, the trial series, the middle of the way — ten skills — and a learner far along — 19 skills, 15 mastered, the review date of one has come. The whole pool per iteration; `ns/task` is a call.
- **Checked** (2026-11-11, AMD Ryzen AI 9 365, 20 threads, 10 runs each, the mean):
  - `Readout.Task` — 697–755 ns per call before the change and 286–346 after, 2.0–2.6 times faster; allocations — 10–12 per call before and one after, the slice of the answer; memory — 465–561 bytes per call before and 24 after;
  - `engine.Choose` on `main` d0bb4d5 — its `BenchmarkChoose` on `olympiad-math` 0.1.1 with a `replace` to this code: 0.96–1.04 ms per choice before and 0.57–0.69 after, 1.5–1.8 times; allocations — 9,538–11,046 before and 1,750–2,558 after, memory 54–63% less. `engine`'s tests on this code are green, the golden choices the same;
  - `just ci-test` — coverage 99.1%; `just ci-lint`; `just ci-proto`; the golden files `testdata/fold/`, `testdata/read/` and `testdata/wire/` — without changes.
  - the job "Gate" of pull request #56 on `sim` `v0.1.8` — all 69 numbers within their bands and to the bit the same as recorded; the session could not run `just gate exact` in the container. The release is `v0.1.6`, commit 7d796e2, checked by `gh release verify` and `release-verify`.

— *Why so:* `sim` will give the gate back 300 learners, and the deciding run 4,000, when the choice becomes faster (Д198, Д199 of `spec`'s RUN.md), and raising the library's version costs it nothing. A change inside touches neither the API nor `engine`: `sim` and `engine` take `v0.1.6` by raising the version, and neither has anything to wait for.

— *Rejected:*
- a new API for many tasks — `Readout.Tasks`, or a task checked once when the pool is loaded: the gain could be larger, but `engine` would need a task of its own and a release of its own, and `sim` would wait for both;
- a lazy cache of retentions in `Readout`, filled by the first `Task`: `Readout` would change after `Read`, and two calls at once would write to one memory (section 3.6, rule 7);
- dropping `Task`'s checks for the modules `Read` read: `Task`'s refusals are part of its contract (section 3.7), and without copies the checks cost little;
- changing `sortedCopy` so that it gives the slice itself: `NewGraph` puts its result into the graph, and the graph would share memory with the caller's input.

— *Consequence:* the merge changes the release's `paths` (R16) and releases `v0.1.6`. `api`'s cache is marked with the model's version (R80 spec), so `api`, having taken it, will recompute the states with the same numbers. `engine` and `sim` learn of the release by a row «в реестр spec»; Д18 is closed. — R07, R09, R12, R16, R20, R25; RUN.md, T40 and Д18; README.md; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.7; `readout.go`, `step.go`, `fold.go`, `codec_check.go`, `graph.go` and the tests.

The round does not wait for the pin of `modules` v0.2.1. The executor takes each task of the epic to the merge into `main` itself, except the drafts of the candidates.** The author's instruction of 2026-11-11 — «создай ветку; в ней отдельным коммитом сделай T38, отдельным коммитом — всё, что можешь, в таблице 4, далее отдельным коммитом архивируй текущий эпик и разворачивай задачи из эпика E5» — after the analysis of E5's readiness, which the author asked for the same day.
