## Catching up after a break

**R31. With the forgetting numbers of the first round the model takes longer to catch up with the truth after a break because it believes in forgetting more strongly: an answer after a pause with a low R̄ says less about the level, and the module's θ is not forgotten over a break, so a fall of the level, which the law of retention does not describe, it puts down to forgetting for longer. This is a property of the step with retention, not a divergence of the model's law from the law of the harness's truth. Forgetting cannot be removed from the step: on populations without a fall the model then takes forgetting for a fall of the level. Catching up is shortened by forgetting of θ — a wider step of θ after a pause of the module of 14 days — and it needs no new field of the state. It goes into the exploration of T43 on its own and together with the forgetting numbers, and the numbers that speed forgetting up are not explored without it.** Task T42 of epic E5 (R30, decision 1); the plan the author approved on 2026-11-11 (RUN.md, T42).
- **The harness** is `sim` `v0.1.8`, commit d957fc7: `engine` `v0.1.6`, `modules` v0.2.0, the set `working`, `engine`'s rule, 1,500 learners per graph of each population — that is the size of the deciding run at this tag (the 2026-11-11 amendment to R33 `sim`) — on both maths. The run is assembled as the recipe `decisive`: a `go.work` in the session's folder with a clone of `sim`, a variant of `learner-model`, and `engine` at the commit of `gate.json`, 20 parts `sim run -populations … -learners 1500 -part k/20 -commit …` and `sim merge`; the comparison is `sim compare` against the base, paired by learner, with a 95-percent interval. The base is `learner-model` `v0.1.4` from `sim`'s `go.mod`: `v0.1.6` gives the same bits (R29). The candidates of the first judgement are the commits of the drafts #43–45: `g10` 7f735b2, `halve035` 17e0a42, `forget-set` 75c6a90. The diagnostic variants are commits of the clone in the session's folder; neither they nor the files of the runs go into the repository, and they were never published anywhere, so a repetition assembles them anew by the description below — then the numbers are the same, and the file's header names its own SHA:
  - `w1`, 7847d28 — `forget-set` and w = 1 for each answer in the step — the field `w` in `answers`, `step.go` — and P with R̄;
  - `nostep`, 4b95d26 — `forget-set` and a step without forgetting: in `answers` P₁ instead of P and w = 1, while τ and reading are as they are; `nostep-base`, 2b69d24 — the same on top of the base;
  - `theta`, 235e44d — the base and forgetting of θ: in `move`, right after `at := e.PracticeTime`, outside the trial series and for a module with skills, if from the greatest t_last of the module's skills to the time of practice is no less than 14 days, n := min(n; 20) — K_θ 0.1 instead of the floor 0.05, and a wider v; `theta-fs`, 3c603ab — the same on top of `forget-set`;
  - `law` — the truth of `F-break` in the clone of `sim`, commit 8c0285c on top of `v0.1.8`, with the law of `forget-set`: `a.Growth = 10` for `F-break` in `internal/population/population.go` and the base 0.35 instead of 0.5 in `internal/learner/attempt.go`.

  The SHA-256 of the files: the base on `F-break` — `12441811f9e5913c…7f5`, and a repetition gave the same file to the byte; the base on `F-model`, `F-none`, `F-exp`, `F-C1` and `F-growth5` — `9a9618339e07ed35…b79`; `g10` — `7bed24d5d11defa9…8f6`, `halve035` — `9db8026222be4046…ef1`, `forget-set` — `1cc325663d9961e4…595`, `w1` — `197998a6d6f68740…a38`, `nostep` — `015a44bf99827aa0…702` and on the five others `8e708bd37d2ea344…54c`, `nostep-base` — `fa5fe8efc0c7d935…f66` and `9e462fa825b97240…997`, `theta` — `a52a27a3c893f1af…320` and `e38b4b538902d076…4fc`, `theta-fs` — `340d89a87bb7a818…d51` and `70161c8e9570f5ba…aba`; on the truth `law` — the base `5a6563097aa625ae…fcf`, `forget-set` `42a776f9ae9c2877…2f4`. A run of `F-break` took from 3 to 10 minutes on 20 cores, of the five other populations from 16 to 38.
- **The yardstick.** "Not worse" is read by the rule of `sim`'s judgement (`notWorseChecks`, `internal/criterion/judge.go` of `sim`): the worse edge of the 95-percent interval of the paired difference is no farther than the tolerance, and the tolerance is the larger of the author's tolerance (`defs`, `measures.go` of `sim`: for `M3.settle` 1 answer, for `M1.rms.200` 0.01, for `M5.caught` 1) and 4.3 standard errors of the difference. The standard error here is taken from the interval of `sim compare`, its width divided by 3.92, and the checks are those the model's criterion puts on the `F-` populations. This is the judgement's yardstick on the `F-` populations alone, not the judgement: T42 did not run the other populations.
- **What the runs showed** — `F-break`, b − a against the base; `M3.settle` — answers until catching up, `M3.unsettled` — the share of those who never caught up, `M3.lag` — the estimate minus the truth from the 101st answer; the last column is the "not worse" checks that the variant fails by the yardstick above, on `F-break` and, where there was a run, on the five other `F-`:

  | Variant | `M3.settle` olympiad | `M3.unsettled` olympiad | `M3.lag` olympiad | `M3.settle` school | "Not worse" failures |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | base | 173.1 | 0.237 | 0.280 | 338.1 | — |
  | `g10` | +13.5 [7.4; 20.1] | +0.011 [−0.011; 0.032] | +0.021 | 0.0 [−1.8; 2.0] | 1: `M3.settle`, 20.09 at a tolerance of 13.91 |
  | `halve035` | +6.3 [−0.7; 13.0] | +0.022 [0.001; 0.043] | +0.014 | +1.8 [−0.6; 4.4] | 0 |
  | `forget-set` | +22.2 [14.9; 29.5] | +0.009 [−0.015; 0.031] | +0.034 | +1.5 [−0.2; 3.3] | 3: `M3.settle`, 29.51 at 15.97; `M1.rms.200` of olympiad; `M5.caught` of school — on the edge |
  | `w1` | +28.0 [20.9; 35.8] | +0.065 [0.040; 0.088] | +0.060 | −3.7 [−7.0; −0.4] | 5 |
  | `nostep` | −51.0 [−58.2; −44.0] | −0.044 [−0.067; −0.022] | −0.262 | −78.5 [−82.4; −74.8] | 3, and 20 on the five others |
  | `nostep-base` | −55.9 [−63.1; −48.7] | −0.040 [−0.064; −0.017] | −0.266 | −78.0 [−81.9; −74.1] | 2: `M6.never` and `E1.corridor` of olympiad, and 19 on the five others |
  | `theta` | −9.8 [−15.0; −4.6] | −0.028 [−0.045; −0.011] | −0.020 | −42.2 [−44.5; −39.8] | 0, and 1 on the five others: `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school, 2.77 at 2.73 |
  | `theta-fs` | +10.0 [3.4; 16.8] | +0.005 [−0.019; 0.028] | +0.016 | −40.4 [−43.2; −37.6] | 1: `M3.settle`, 16.8 at 14.71; and 2 on the five others |

  On the truth `law`, which forgets by the law of `forget-set`, the base catches up in 162.1 answers, and `forget-set` 14.9 [8.2; 21.6] longer; on school maths 3.7 [1.1; 6.4] longer. The first judgement failed `g10` and `halve035` on `F-break` at 1,000 learners, on `engine` `v0.1.4` (R37 `sim`); here `halve035` holds the check: the harness is different — `engine` `v0.1.6` and the packages v0.2.0 — and that is for the judgement to decide, not the analysis.
- **Why so.** The step of θ is K_θ · w · (S − P), where P = c + (1 − c) · R̄ · σ ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.2). The lower R̄, the less P depends on θ — the factor R̄ at σ — and the smaller w, so an answer after a pause moves θ toward the truth the more weakly, the more strongly the model believes in forgetting. After the break of `F-break` the truth's level falls by 1.0, while the module's θ is not forgotten: the model sees the fall only as wrong answers and, while R̄ is low, explains them by forgetting. The numbers of the first round grow τ more slowly and cut it harder, their R̄ after pauses is lower — and catching up is slower. Their calibration of P after pauses of 30 days and more is better at that: the bias `M2.bias.pause30` of the base is +0.048, of `forget-set` −0.011 — this is the same shift: the fall of the level is explained by forgetting, not by the level. What does not explain it:
  1. the weight w — `w1` catches up even longer: w damps the correct answer after a pause too, which with a low P would pull θ up;
  2. a divergence of the model's law from the law of the truth: the truth of `F-break` forgets by the law of the base — g = 15 and halving (`internal/learner/attempt.go` of `sim`) — and this would give the candidate an extra delay, but on the truth `law`, with the law of `forget-set` itself, the delay is 14.9 answers of 22.2: most of it comes from the numbers themselves. So there is no row «в реестр spec» for `sim`: `F-break` judges what it should.

  And the long catching up of the base itself — 173 answers, and a quarter of the learners of olympiad maths do not catch up — comes from θ not being forgotten and from the step of θ at n > 60 standing at the floor 0.05: `theta`, with a wider step after a pause, shortens it. So the condition of R30, decision 2, is met: the delay comes from θ not forgetting over a break, which the step with retention after a pause hardly moves — and forgetting of θ goes into the exploration.
- **A step without forgetting is rejected.** `nostep-base` catches up on `F-break` 56 answers faster, but on populations without a fall of the level it errs the other way — it takes forgetting for a fall of the level, and θ goes below the truth: on olympiad maths `M3.lag` on `F-model` is −0.27 instead of −0.02, and `M1.rms.200` is worse by 0.14 on `F-model`, 0.19 on `F-exp`, 0.29 on `F-C1` and 0.23 on `F-growth5`, and by 0.02 on `F-C1` of school maths. On `F-none` of olympiad maths and on `F-model` and `F-none` of school maths its error is, on the contrary, smaller: there the truth does not forget, or forgets more slowly than the model believes. By the judgement's yardstick — 19 failures on the five other `F-` and 2 on `F-break`.
- **Forgetting of θ.** `theta` costs little on the five other `F-`, but not nothing: `M6.never` on olympiad maths is worse by 0.002–0.003 where it is outside the noise — on `F-model`, `F-none` and `F-exp` — v is wider, and "mastered" comes later; Brier and `M10.retention` are worse by less than a tenth of the tolerance; and one check fails by a hair — `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths, +1.5 [0.3; 2.8] answers at the judgement's tolerance of 2.73. Better are the false "mastered" on `F-none` and `F-C1` of olympiad maths and `M5.caught` on `F-exp` and `F-C1` of school maths. With `forget-set` the rule gives catching up back 12 answers of 22, but `theta-fs` still fails the check `M3.settle`. On the five other `F-`, `theta-fs` keeps the gain of `forget-set` in forgetting — `E3.late` is better by 0.03–0.12 on olympiad maths and by 0.29–0.36 on school maths, except `F-growth5`, where it is within the noise, and `M10.retention` on olympiad maths by 0.001–0.005 — but fails `M1.rms.200` on `F-none` of olympiad maths, 0.0204 at 0.014 — `forget-set` had the same failure in the first judgement — and `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths, 3.65 at 3.41. Worse than the base outside the noise it also has `M10.retention` on school maths — on `F-none` by 0.0014, two thirds of the author's tolerance, on `F-model` and `F-exp` by 0.0004–0.0005 — and `M1.rms.200` on `F-model`, `F-none` and `F-growth5` of school maths, by 0.0001–0.0006.
- **No field is needed.** The time of the module's last practice is the greatest t_last of its skills (`SkillState.LastPractice`, `state.go`): each piece of evidence that moves a skill sets its t_last. The rule, which acts on the first piece of evidence after a pause, reads it, and it lasts through n, which is already in the state. The price is the meaning of n: the "number of answers" becomes smaller after a pause, and with it, for each skill of the module, not only the one that came back, v is wider and the cautious level λ lower. `engine` reads this too — the estimate, the uncertainty and "mastered" through `Readout` — so the exploration measures the corridor `E1.corridor` and the measures `E2` too, not "mastered" and the review date alone: on `F-growth5` of olympiad maths `theta` has already shifted the corridor by 0.001, within the tolerance. A candidate with the rule changes [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.1 and 3.2, and the state kind stays 1. An edge: a piece of evidence whose answers have no primary skills in the graph, J = 0, moves θ but not t_last — then the module's pause is counted from the last practice of the skills, not from it; T44 checks this with a test. A new field — a separate step of θ after a pause that does not touch n and v — is needed only if the exploration shows that the price of n does not hold "not worse"; that is state kind 2 and a recomputation of `api`'s cache (R08; R80 spec), and the author decides it in the planning of T44 (R30, decision 2).
- **What was decided:**
  1. the cause of the delay is the step with retention with a θ that is not forgotten, not the numbers on their own and not the law of the harness's truth: numbers that speed forgetting up always delay catching up, and in T43 they are not explored without forgetting of θ;
  2. forgetting of θ goes into the exploration of T43 — n := min(n; N) after a pause of the module of no less than d₀ days — with two numbers: N, the strength, and d₀, the threshold of the pause. The diagnostics took N = 20 and d₀ = 14 — the threshold of a skill's pause in `sim`'s measures; the exploration looks for N and d₀ for the rule on its own and together with g and the base of the division of τ — in a set with `forget-set` the rule must give catching up back all 22 answers, not 12, and leave no failure of `M1.rms.200` on `F-none` of olympiad maths — and measures it on all populations, with `E1.corridor` and `E2`, and `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths;
  3. rejected: a step without forgetting — it breaks the error of the estimate on four of the five `F-` without a fall on olympiad maths and on `F-C1` of school maths; w = 1 — it catches up worse; a row «в реестр spec» for `sim` about the law of the truth of `F-break` — the delay comes from the numbers themselves, not from a divergence of the law.

— *Why so:* the judgement found the failure but not its cause (R37 `sim`), and an exploration without the cause would again choose the forgetting numbers that delay catching up. The runs went only on the `F-` populations — the break and the measures of forgetting are there — so the analysis took hours, not a day. The other populations know pauses too — a session a day with probability 5/7, and a skill waits its turn for days — and T42 did not measure the price of the rules on them: the exploration of T43 measures it on all populations.

— *Rejected:*
- small runs on 300 learners, as in the exploration of the first round: 4.3 standard errors of the difference of `M3.settle` at 1,500 learners are 14–16 answers, and at 300 twice as many, wider than the difference itself (the 2026-11-10 amendment to R24);
- tracing the step through the answers after a pause: the runs told the hypotheses apart without it (the plan of T42);
- a field "time of the module's last practice": it is derived from the skills.

— *Consequence:* nothing changes in the code, the tests and the documents of how it is built. RUN.md — T42 and the input of T43, the row Д6; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 2.2, "Deferred". — R04, R22, R24 and its 2026-11-10 amendment, R30; R35, R37 `sim`; 3.2 and 3.5 `sim`; Д6.

## The exploration of E5

**R32. One candidate goes to the judgement `model-2` — `theta`, forgetting of θ: n := min(n; 20) after a pause of the module of no less than 14 days, without a new field, the group `jumps`, without a target, two constants. It was chosen by the exploration of T43 — deciding runs of the harness at 2,000 working learners per cell, on all populations — the base and nine variants: forgetting of θ with N 10, 20 and 40 at d₀ 14 and with d₀ 7 and 28 at N 20, the same together with the forgetting numbers of the first round, and a weight of 1/2 for an answer with help. The rule with N = 20 and d₀ = 14 holds all the constraints, is better than the base and shortens catching up after a break. With it the forgetting numbers no longer delay catching up, but none of their sets holds all the constraints: `forget-set` still breaks the error of the estimate and the corridor — most of all outside the `F-` populations, where the rule does not act, but on `F-none` too — and the base of the division of τ alone — both 0.35 and 0.4 — fails one check each. The weight 1/2 for an answer with help is rejected.** The author's decisions of 2026-11-12 in the planning of T43 — the make-up: forgetting of θ and one run of the weight of an answer with help, and not to repeat the step of the bands, σ₀, I, ρ and τ₀ (R24); about ten runs with stopping rules — and in the execution: one more run, the rule of θ with the base 0.4, after `thh20-14` failed one check by a hair. The executor then miscounted and called the budget exhausted, while the run was the tenth, within the budget (RUN.md, T43).
- **The harness** is `sim` `v0.1.10`, commit ff921be, the tag the gate stands on (the 2026-11-12 amendment to R20): `learner-model` v0.1.6, `engine` v0.1.6, the packages of `modules` v0.2.5 with `olympiad-math` and `grade-3-math` 0.3.0 — with `time` and leading questions — the set `working`, `engine`'s rule, all populations. A run is `sim`'s recipe `just decisive engine <file>`, and a variant is `CANDIDATE_LEARNER_MODEL` with the folder of the variant's working copy: the recipe keeps the build by `gate.json`, and the file names the commit. The base is a run without a candidate, by `sim`'s `go.mod`: SHA-256 `7db81ec7bdf8c02a…92e` — exactly `base` in `internal/criterion/paths.json` of `sim`, so the exploration's base is the same one on which the judgement reads the paths. The variants are commits on top of `v0.1.6` (7d796e2) in a clone in the session's folder; neither they nor the files of the runs go into the repository, and they were never published anywhere, so a repetition assembles them anew by the description below — then the numbers are the same, and the file's header names its own SHA:
  - the rule of θ is in `move` (`step.go`), right after `at := e.PracticeTime`: outside the trial series and for a module with skills, if `pauseDays` from the greatest `LastPractice` of the module's skills to `at` is no less than `thetaPause`, then `AnswerWeight` := min(`AnswerWeight`; `thetaWeight`); both constants are in `model.go`, after `secondsPerDay`. The same as `theta` of R31, but with d₀ and N as constants. N no less than 5 is required: n < 5 would return the module to the trial series (`inTrial`);
  - `thN-d` is the rule with N and d₀: `th10-14` e47c860, `th20-14` 90b6236, `th40-14` 796d781, `th20-7` c7aaba7, `th20-28` 4268e96;
  - `thfs20-14`, 4cd32e6 — `th20-14` and `forget-set`: `stabilityGrowth` 10 and the base of the division of τ 0.35 in `stabilityAfter`; `thh20-14`, 6e36283 — `th20-14` and the base 0.35 alone; `thh040-20-14`, 011f089 — `th20-14` and the base 0.4;
  - `help05`, d98e1d6 — the weight w of an answer with help (`helped`) multiplied by 1/2 in `answers`, so that its pull in the step of θ and δ is half as large, as the variant of T30 (R24).

  The SHA-256 of the files: `th10-14` — `3103286a394584c8…8e0`, `th20-14` — `356b4969438ce09f…bef`, `th40-14` — `73164447c7dd9367…b5e`, `th20-7` — `77313baeea2d8ad6…df8`, `th20-28` — `fb3651b2c39b706f…3e1`, `thfs20-14` — `329f803c8aeca9fb…3dc`, `thh20-14` — `4994fd294ed902b6…9e9`, `thh040-20-14` — `1ea79fcaa4b28187…623`, `help05` — `1149cd8721bcda8b…801`. A run took from 36 to 41 minutes on 20 cores, and when neighbours shared the host, 50 minutes.
- **The yardstick** is the judgement `sim judge` with the model's criterion by a judgement file in the session's folder: the base and the variants, each with its group and number of constants. At 2,000 learners it reads as a deciding run: "This is a decisive run". But this is the exploration, not the judgement: the judgement `model-2` runs the same working learners with the same seeds, so on a commit with the same code it will repeat their numbers number for number — the file itself differs by the header, which names the commit — and the confirmation on the untouched `held-out-model-1` (R35 `sim`), which the exploration did not touch, guards against fitting to the working learners. The differences by measures are `sim compare` against the base; below, b − a, with a 95-percent interval.
- **What the runs showed** — the estimate and the group's score with an interval, the "not worse" failures by the judgement's rule; `M3.settle` — answers until catching up on `F-break`, for the base 161.1 on olympiad maths and 336.4 on school maths; the last column is `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths, where the rule costs most:

  | Variant | Estimate | Group | Failures | `M3.settle` olympiad | `M3.settle` school | `M5.caught` `F-none` school |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | `th10-14` | +0.0127 [0.0075; 0.0179] | `jumps` +0.035 | 1: `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school, 3.54 at 2.61 | −20.7 [−25.5; −16.2] | −75.0 [−77.6; −72.4] | +2.29 [1.14; 3.54] |
  | `th20-14` | +0.0103 [0.0052; 0.0152] | `jumps` +0.021 [0.018; 0.023] | 0 | −12.7 [−17.0; −8.3] | −42.6 [−44.7; −40.5] | +1.26 [0.19; 2.35] |
  | `th40-14` | +0.0063 [0.0022; 0.0101] | `jumps` +0.004 | 0 | −3.2 [−7.2; 0.9] | −7.9 [−8.9; −6.7] | +0.49 [−0.33; 1.26] |
  | `th20-7` | +0.0083 [0.0024; 0.0146] | `jumps` +0.021 | 1: `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school, 2.69 at 2.58 | −13.3 [−18.1; −8.7] | −44.3 [−46.5; −42.2] | +1.47 [0.34; 2.69] |
  | `th20-28` | +0.0068 [0.0025; 0.0111] | `jumps` +0.015 | 0 | −9.0 [−12.6; −5.3] | −31.3 [−33.2; −29.3] | +0.45 [−0.49; 1.35] |
  | `thfs20-14` | +0.0361 [0.0269; 0.0461] | `forgets` +0.277 [0.207; 0.352] | 7: `E1.corridor` on G2, G3 and S-adult and `M1.rms.200` on G3-drop, S-scale1.5 and `F-none` of olympiad, `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school, 3.05 at 3.01 | +4.5 [−1.2; 10.0] | −38.7 [−41.6; −35.6] | +1.63 [0.30; 3.05] |
  | `thh20-14` | +0.0161 [0.0065; 0.0262] | `forgets` +0.119 [0.045; 0.197] | 1: `M1.rms.200` on G8 of olympiad, 0.01044 at 0.01 | −5.4 [−10.7; −0.1] | −41.0 [−43.6; −38.5] | +1.80 [0.22; 3.47] |
  | `thh040-20-14` | +0.0092 [−0.0003; 0.0190] | `forgets` +0.067 [−0.007; 0.141] | 1: `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school, 4.83 at 3.75 | −4.0 [−9.4; 1.3] | −42.5 [−44.9; −40.1] | +3.07 [1.46; 4.83] |
  | `help05` | −0.0297 [−0.0411; −0.0188] | `learns` −0.080 [−0.082; −0.078] | 62: 46 on school, 13 on olympiad, 2 on the pair of maths, 1 on essays | +4.5 [−0.4; 9.1] | +350.0 [343.0; 357.4] | −2.45 [−4.41; −0.59] |

  "Group" is the score of the group the variant named as its own: the rule of θ — `jumps`, the sets with the forgetting numbers — `forgets`, the weight of help — `learns`; for `thfs20-14` the score in the group `jumps` is +0.008 [−0.010; 0.028] — within the noise. The exploration's judgement of the first stage called `th20-14` and `th40-14` equal to the best, and chose `th20-14`.
- **The price of `th20-14`.** The rule moves numbers only on the six `F-` populations and four numbers on `A-fading`: on the other populations a pause of the module of 14 days does not come, and the files coincide with the base number for number. The corridor `E1.corridor` and the loops `E2` are shifted outside the noise nowhere, so `engine`, which reads the estimate, the uncertainty and "mastered" through `Readout`, pays almost no price. Better are the false "mastered" after a break `M4.false.break` on olympiad maths, by 0.005–0.034 on `F-model`, `F-none`, `F-exp`, `F-growth5` and `F-break`, `M1.rms.200` on `F-break` of olympiad maths, by 0.012, and `M5.caught` on `F-exp` of school maths, by 0.55 answers. Worse are `M6.never` on olympiad maths, by 0.0025–0.0036 on `F-model`, `F-none`, `F-C1`, `F-growth5` and `F-break`: v is wider, and "mastered" comes later — and `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths, by 1.26 [0.19; 2.35] answers at the judgement's tolerance of 2.61. Brier `M2` and `M10.retention` are shifted by less than 0.001 — Brier by less than half of the author's tolerance, 0.002. This is what R31 found for `theta` on the `F-` populations of `sim` `v0.1.8`, and on `v0.1.10` the check `M5.caught` holds.
- **Why so.**
  1. The strength N: the smaller N, the faster catching up and the dearer `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths — the answers of a skill after its pause of 14 days or more until the P of the reference task stays within 0.05 of the truth for five answers in a row (3.5 `sim`). Resetting n makes the step of θ after a pause wider, while on `F-none` the truth does not forget and the level does not fall, so a wide step catches up nothing there and only makes P swing around the truth for longer. N 10 fails this check, N 40 hardly moves catching up, N 20 is between them.
  2. The threshold d₀: 7 days catch up a little faster than 14, but fire on ordinary pauses of skills and fail the same check; 28 days are weaker and no cheaper. The threshold of a skill's pause in `sim`'s measures is the same 14 days (R31).
  3. The forgetting numbers: with the rule of θ, catching up for `forget-set` is within the noise of the base, +4.5 [−1.2; 10.0] answers, whereas without it R31 on `sim` `v0.1.8` saw a delay of 22.2 answers: the delay that R31 explained by the belief in forgetting with a θ that is not forgotten, the rule removes. But the failures of `forget-set` in the first judgement outside the `F-` populations — `E1.corridor` on G2 and S-adult, `M1.rms.200` on G3-drop and S-scale1.5 (R37 `sim`) — remained: the rule of θ does not act there, so they come from the growth of τ at g 10 itself. Remained too the failure that the rule was to remove by R31 — `M1.rms.200` on `F-none` of olympiad maths, 0.01196 at 0.01125 — and with it `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths. The base of the division of τ alone breaks the error of the estimate less: at 0.35 — `M1.rms.200` on G8 of olympiad maths, 0.01044 at a tolerance of 0.01, 4% beyond it; at 0.4 G8 holds, 0.0088, but `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths fails, 4.83 at 3.75. This measure on this cell is noisier than the others — the standard error of the difference from 0.6 to 0.9 answers — and at 0.35 it was +1.80, and at 0.4 +3.07: of the two bases neither gives a set that holds everything.
  4. The weight 1/2 for an answer with help: with the leading questions of the packages v0.2.5, help is each answer of an attempt where the button "Hint" gave a question (R28), and the half weight damps these pieces of evidence in the step of θ and δ; what share of the answers they make up, the exploration did not measure. Worst of all is school maths: `M1.rms.200` on G2 is worse by 0.68, `M3.lag` on G2-half by 0.31, catching up on `F-break` by 350 answers. The direction and the strength are the same as in the exploration of T30 on packages without leading questions: `learns` there −0.086, here −0.080 (R24) — so leading questions do not change the conclusion.
- **What was decided:**
  1. one candidate goes to the judgement `model-2` — `theta`: the rule of θ with N = 20 and d₀ = 14, as the variant `th20-14`, the group `jumps`, no target — the rule does not move the share of those who did not catch up on G3 or the lag on G2-half — two constants, no new field, the state kind stays 1. It is one idea with one set of numbers. The exploration's judgement called `th40-14` equal by the estimate, but of the two chose `th20-14`, and `th40-14` does not shift catching up on `F-break` of olympiad maths outside the noise, while that is the round's target (R30). If the confirmation of `theta` does not pass, the judgement does not try a second candidate on the same held-out learners (R35 `sim`), so `th40-14` as a second candidate would add no choice;
  2. rejected: `th10-14` and `th20-7` — they fail `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths; `th40-14` and `th20-28` — they hold everything, but are weaker than `th20-14`; `thfs20-14` — seven failures outside catching up; `thh20-14` and `thh040-20-14` — one failure each, and the judgement runs the same learners with the same seeds and will repeat it number for number. The rule of the tolerance — the larger of the author's tolerance and 4.3 standard errors of the difference of this candidate — was recorded before the first candidate and does not change for a candidate (R04 `sim`), even though the tolerance itself depends on the noise of its difference: for `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths it is from 2.58 to 3.75; `help05` — 62 failures;
  3. Д5 and ρ, τ₀ of Д6 and Д7 were not explored — the author's decision, R24: there are no new facts about them.

— *Why so:* an exploration at the size of the deciding run and on the same harness as the judgement is the lesson of the first round: the coarse look at 300 learners did not see the failures the judgement found (the 2026-11-10 amendment to R24). The price is that the judgement `model-2` of `theta` is already known on the working learners, and the confirmation on the held-out ones decides.

— *What the exploration does not decide:* the judgement and the confirmation are `sim`'s tasks (T45). On essays the rule shifted no numbers — the files of `th20-14` and of the base coincide there. A set that keeps the gain of `forget-set` in forgetting without failures outside `F-` — a base between 0.4 and 0.5, or a growth of τ that slows down with τ itself ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 2.2, "Deferred") — is a question for the next round.

— *Rejected:*
- a candidate for forgetting — a set of the rule of θ with the forgetting numbers — despite the failure by a hair: the judgement will repeat it, and the candidate's exit would give nothing but a run of the judgement;
- more runs on the base of the division of τ — 0.45 and closer to the base: the author allotted about ten runs to the exploration, and the tenth went to the base 0.4, while `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths is so noisy that the exploration cannot find the boundary between 0.4 and 0.5 without several runs per point;
- an exploration on the `F-` populations alone, then a full run for the finalists: the price of the rule outside `F-` for the finalists would be visible too, but a full run of all the variants cost as many hours, and "not worse" was read on all populations at once.

— *Consequence:* T44 writes the draft pull request `theta` — the rule with N and d₀ as named constants, as the variant `th20-14`, tests and golden files — and compares the run of `just decisive` on its commit with the file of `th20-14`. In the variant the rule stands before the check "no answers" in `move`, so a piece of evidence without answers after a pause narrows n too, while [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.5, requires that such a piece of evidence change nothing. The candidate puts the rule after this check: `answers` does not read n, so the answers of a piece of evidence with answers are the same. If the numbers of the candidate's run do diverge from `th20-14` after all, the judgement reads the candidate's run, and R32 gets an amendment. T44 checks the edge J = 0 of R31 with a test. The rows Д5–Д7 of table 4 of RUN.md — what the exploration learned; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 2.2, "Deferred". — R04, R22, R24, R31; R35, R37 `sim`; RUN.md, T43, T44, Д5–Д7.

— *Amendment 2026-11-13:* **the judgement `model-2` chose `theta`, and the confirmation on `held-out-model-1` passed: on the working learners it repeated the exploration number for number, and on the held-out ones `theta` holds all the constraints and is better than the base. The chosen one came out by the merge of T45 (the second 2026-11-13 amendment to R04).** The judgement is T47 of `sim`'s RUN.md, R46 `sim`; the report `judgements/model-2/report.md` of `sim` at `v0.1.13`, read 2026-11-13. The deciding runs are 2,000 learners per cell, `sim` after `v0.1.12`, on the candidate's commit a063835:
  - the working learners: the estimate +0.01029 [0.005208; 0.01522], the group `jumps` +0.02052 [0.01775; 0.02325], no "not worse" failures — the same numbers as `th20-14` in the table above. The SHA-256 of the candidate's file is `35051542b9fdaf49…7c19`, the same to the byte as the run of T44 on `sim` `v0.1.10`: the run was repeated on another clone and another tag of `sim`;
  - `held-out-model-1`, seed 20261108: the estimate +0.04215 [0.03279; 0.05219], the group `jumps` +0.02025 [0.01765; 0.02294], no failures. The estimate is four times higher than on the working learners, and the score of its own group the same; the confirmation does not require a repetition of the estimate (R46 `sim`);
  - both sets have the same measures that left the estimate: the path not read or not above zero — `M7.hard` on G1 and S-adult, `M4.false.break` on four `F-` and `M4.false.pages` on three `W-` of school maths — the path within the author's tolerance — three measures of school maths and `M9.hasFalse` on A-unpractised of the pair of maths; the base reads no measures of essays, and on the held-out ones also `M4.false.all` on G0-start0.5 of school maths.

  `sim`'s base, paths and bands are now on the commit of `theta`, and `held-out-model-1` is no longer held out: the next round of the model is confirmed on `held-out-model-2` (R46 `sim`). — RUN.md, T45; Д6.

— *Second amendment 2026-11-13 (R37):* the next round that "What the exploration does not decide" speaks of is epic E7, T51–T55 of RUN.md: the base of the division of τ between 0.4 and 0.5 and a growth of τ that slows down with τ, on the base with `theta`.

— *Amendment 2026-11-16 (R40):* that round did not run: T52–T55 are deferred, `[?]`, since `sim` holds no rounds by plan (R59 sim), and come back when a candidate of forgetting brings a cause of its own; E7 is in [plan/E7.md](plan/E7.md). ## A shorter RUN.md

**R36. RUN.md keeps only what is live and what no other file says: the executor's rules, the current epic, the tables 2 and 3, and the bodies of the tasks not yet done. The section «Задачи вне эпика» is gone: a task outside an epic stands in table 1 of the current epic and goes into its archive like the others. Rules that repeat RULES.md or CLAUDE.md are a line with a pointer, and their numbers stay. The history of the tables and the section «Задачи вне эпика» went to the archive of the last epic done word for word, not lost. This is the shape `sim` gave its RUN.md by R47 `sim`.** The author's instructions of 2026-11-13: «почисти файл RUN.md от ненужной для тебя информации чтобы уменьшить потребление токенов», «удали Задачи вне эпика», and, when E7 was unfolded, «как R47 sim» — the answer to whether to bring the cleanup to the shape of R47 `sim`.
- **What changed** (RUN.md, 37,869 bytes → 18,871 at commit 5d37495, before E7 was unfolded):
  - the opening — without the paragraph on the epics done and without the repeat of "one task is one session";
  - rules 3, 5 and 10 — a pointer to RULES.md, Hard rules and How we work; rule 6 — without the list of what `docs-check` checks, which is in CLAUDE.md, Environment; rule 2 — the rules of the parts in `.claude/rules/`; rule 9 — a task outside an epic stands in table 1, and without a current epic goes to the archive of the last one done, as R35 says; the statuses `[-]` and `[?]` stay the author's decision; the block «Ссылки» — gone, as RULES.md, Citations says it;
  - «Статусы», «Кто», «План» and «Тело задачи» — a line each; «Как устроен план: эпики» — the same rules, shorter; the list of done epics — one line each;
  - the list of done epics as it was, the opening paragraphs of tables 2–4 — the history of the epics proposed, of the closed rows В1–В48 and of the move of table 4 — and the section «Задачи вне эпика» with the phrases of T48–T50 — to [plan/E5.md](plan/E5.md), «Из RUN.md». Table 3 keeps its next number, В50, which `docs-check` reads.

— *Why so:* every session reads RUN.md whole, at its start, and half of it was history or a copy of the shared rules that CLAUDE.md already loads. The template of `spec` keeps the section «Задачи вне эпика» (R19 spec, R145 spec), but here it held only the finished T48–T50, whose bodies were in the archive already; a task outside an epic loses nothing by standing in table 1. `sim` made the same choice the same day, and one shape in both repositories is cheaper to read than two.

— *Rejected:*
- keeping the section empty, or creating it with the first task outside an epic — such a task can stand in table 1 like any other;
- deleting the history without moving it, as the first pass of the cleanup did — the archive of the epic is where it would have gone at the next epic's end, and the decisions cite it;
- renumbering the rules — the log and CLAUDE.md cite them by number.

— *Consequence:* the second 2026-11-13 amendment to R35. A move to a newer tag of `spec` that brings a new RUN.md of the template reads this entry first. The documents are outside the release's `paths`: the merge releases no tag (R16). — RUN.md; [plan/E5.md](plan/E5.md), «Из RUN.md».

— *Amendment 2026-11-16 (R39):* **RUN.md keeps its rules and the current epic, not tables 2 and 3, which are `run/EPICS.md` and `run/QUESTIONS.md` (R153 spec), and a task outside an epic no longer stands in table 1: it is a body of `run/TASKS.md` until done, and then goes to the current epic's archive, section «Задачи вне эпика, сделанные за его время», or with no current epic to the last done one's, «… после него» (R155 spec). T56 and T57 keep their rows in table 1 of E7. Rules 6 and 10 still point to CLAUDE.md and RULES.md, as this entry decided; rules 3 and 5 are the template's text.** — RUN.md, T57.

— *Amendment 2026-11-17 (R46):* **rule 10 is still a pointer to RULES.md, "Hard rules", with one clause that RULES.md does not say: a commit message or a pull request's description is never a copy of the report — the template's rule 10 of `spec` v0.18.14 says it, and its other words on commits and pull requests are RULES.md's "Language" (the 2026-11-17 amendment to R14 spec).** — RUN.md, rule 10; T70.

## Epic E7

**R37. Epic E7 is the third round of the model's candidates: the forgetting numbers of R04 on the base with `theta`. First the gate on the latest tag of `sim`, then the exploration at the size of the deciding run on that same tag, the drafts of the candidates, the judgement `model-3` with its confirmation on `held-out-model-2`, and the summary — T51–T55. The tag of `sim` stays the same from T51 to the judgement's choice. Each task is a branch of its own from `main`, which the executor takes to the merge, except the drafts of the candidates.** The author's instruction of 2026-11-13, «разворачивай задачи E7», after the analysis of what E7 is for and whether it can be taken; in the planning — a branch per task, as in E4 and in E5 from T44. The make-up is by the row of E7 in table 2 of RUN.md, which T46 proposed.
- **What was found** (2026-11-13; `sim` `main` 9c8f782 and `engine` `main`, through `gh api`):
  - `sim` has done T48: its base is the release `v0.1.8`, commit ab688b2, at the tag `v0.1.14`, and its E5 waits only for its summary. R46 `sim` names the next round of the model — `judgements/model-3/`, confirmed on `held-out-model-2` — but no task of `sim` judges it yet, and table 2 of `sim` holds only E6, the judgement `engine-2`;
  - `engine` is in its E7, "Lesson consolidation": its T52 waits for a tag of `sim` with the step and the criterion of its T50, and its T53 for a judgement of `sim`. The candidates of `engine`'s second round of the corridor, which `engine-2` judges, do not exist yet;
  - "Gate" here stands on `sim` `v0.1.13`, commit 7c32016, whose base is the commit of `theta` a063835; `v0.1.14` has the same base on the release;
  - the growth of τ is τ · (1 + g · (1 − R)) for a right answer and halving for a wrong one (`stabilityAfter`, `model.go`); in FSRS the gain of stability falls with stability (sources 3.1). A growth that depends on τ itself needs no new field: τ is in the state, and the state kind stays 1.
- **What was decided:**
  1. the tasks T51–T55: the gate on the latest tag of `sim` (T51); the exploration (T52) — the base of the division of τ between 0.4 and 0.5 and a growth of τ that slows down with τ, alone and with g, measured on the checks that the sets of T43 failed; the drafts (T53), the judgement and the release (T54), the summary (T55) — as T43–T46 of E5;
  2. no analysis task as T42: R32 already names the cause of the failures of `forget-set` outside `F-` — the growth of τ at g 10, on which the rule of θ does not act;
  3. a branch per task from `main`, a pull request and a merge that the executor takes to green CI itself (R30, decision 5); the drafts of the candidates are branches from `main` and wait for the judgement, and T54 merges the chosen one, which releases a patch of the line `0.1`;
  4. the tag of `sim` is held from T51 to the judgement's choice: the numbers of the exploration equal the judgement's on the working learners only on the same tag (R32). Who holds `sim`'s pin first — this round, `engine`'s lesson consolidation or `engine-2` — is the row В50 «в реестр spec».

— *Why so:* the gate before the exploration is the order of E4 and E5 (T36, T41): a red "Gate" on a candidate must mean a shift of the measure, not a new tag. The exploration at the size of the deciding run on the judgement's tag is the lesson of the first round (the 2026-11-10 amendment to R24) and gave the judgement `model-2` number for number (the 2026-11-13 amendment to R32).

— *Rejected:*
- an analysis task as T42 — the cause is known, and an analysis would add no fact the exploration needs;
- one branch of the epic, as E5 went until T44 — the author chose a branch per task; the code of the round is only the drafts of T53, and one of them goes into `main`, so the round comes out as one release either way;
- the exploration on `sim` `v0.1.13`, without T51 — the judgement goes on the latest tag, and a later tag would shift the exploration's numbers;
- Д5, ρ, τ₀ and the weight of an answer with help in the round — there are no new facts about them (R24, R32).

— *Consequence:* RUN.md — E7 in table 1 with its tasks, the row of E7 has left table 2, and the row В50; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 2.2, "Deferred"; [DEBT.md](../run/DEBT.md), Д6; the second 2026-11-13 amendment to R32. The documents are outside the release's `paths`: the merge releases no tag (R16). — R04, R24, R30, R32; R46 `sim`; RUN.md, table 1, В50.

— *Amendment 2026-11-16 (R38):* **`spec` answered В50 with row 4.17 of its `COORDINATION.md`: there is no judgement `model-3` in `sim`'s queue, `sim` opens a round as an epic once the library brings candidates with their cause (R59 sim), and the next round of the model is the fairness by the grade (R58 sim). T52–T55 go `[?]` and E7 goes to its archive with T51 — the author's decision of 2026-11-15, which the next task of epic E14 of `spec` carries out here (T171 spec), not the move of R38.** Until then T52 stands where it stopped: its variants built, no run made. В50 has left table 3.

— *Second amendment 2026-11-16 (R40):* **carried out by T58: T52–T55 are `[?]` — back when a candidate of forgetting brings a cause of its own (R59 sim; row В16 of `sim`) —, E7 with T51 is in [plan/E7.md](plan/E7.md), and the current epic is E8, the round of fairness by the grade.** Decision 1's judgement `model-3` with its confirmation on `held-out-model-2` and decision 4's tag of `sim` held to the judgement's choice no longer hold: there is no such judgement. Decision 3 holds for E8 too, but for the merge in a run of the orchestrator (R40, decision 5).

## Epic E8

**R40. Epic E8 is the round of fairness by the grade: why the model leaves a start above the truth more slowly than it rises from a start below it, then candidates that bring `M11.same` of `D-grade5` and `D-grade1` up to 0.90 under the model's criterion as is, judged by `sim`'s E10 on the candidate's commit, and the chosen one released or the exit recorded, and the outcome «в реестр spec» for dpia 10.2 spec and dpia 8 spec — T59–T64. T52–T55 of E7 are deferred, `[?]`, and E7 is in its archive with T51.** The author's decision of 2026-11-15 (R156 spec, "Row 4.17"; RUN.md spec, E14), carried out by T58, a task outside an epic written by the text that `spec`'s orchestrator gave for row 4.17 of its `COORDINATION.md` (rule 6 of `spec`'s `COORDINATION.md`). The split into tasks is the executor's: in a run of the orchestrator nobody approves a plan, and its questions are rows of table 3 (R156 spec).
- **What was found** (2026-11-16; `sim` `main` c5e9a4c, through `gh api` and a clone outside the repository):
  - the evaluation `fairness-1` of `sim`, on the base `learner-model` v0.1.8 and `engine` v0.1.6: under grades 1 and 5 of olympiad maths, where the model starts a band below and a band above the one truth, the estimate comes to that under grade 3 by the 50th answer for 0.812 [0.795; 0.829] and 0.5525 [0.53; 0.5735] of the learners, against the bound 0.90, after 32.5 and 83.3 answers on average, a learner who never comes within the run of 200 answers counting as 200; the bands of the tasks hold. "Why the start above the truth is left more slowly is not measured here; it is a question for `learner-model`" (R58 sim). `fair` holds a candidate no worse than the base but does not reward a better one (R55 sim);
  - `sim` holds no rounds by plan, and `model-3` has left its queue; the one judgement in it is this round, E10 of its table 2, laid out once `learner-model` takes the round, with its criterion written down before the first candidate — the model's criterion as is, the target `M11.same` of `D-grade1` and `D-grade5`, its own folder and held-out set, as for `engine-lesson-1` (R49, R52, R59 sim; row 4.18 of `spec`'s `COORDINATION.md`). Its latest tag is `v0.1.21`, with the bits of `v0.1.20` (R60 sim); "Gate" here is on `v0.1.14`;
  - the start is θ₀, the offset of the grade's band; while the weight of answers is below 5, θ is the maximum of the trial series' posterior with the prior N(θ₀, σ₀²), σ₀ = 2.5; then the step of Elo with K_θ = max(0.2 / (1 + 0.05 · n), 0.05) (R03). These are numbers a candidate changes without a new field. A start other than the grade's band changes R51 spec — "A learner of a grade starts in the band of their grade" — and concepts 11 spec;
  - σ₀ 3.0 was explored in T30, at 300 learners and before `M11` existed: no better than the base on the overall score, with 28 constraints violated, but `off-band` — the learners whose grade is off their level — up by +0.062 beyond the noise, while σ₀ 2.0 worsened it by −0.217 (R24, decision 2; Д5).

T59 and T60 do not wait for each other, and run one at a time, in either order, as tasks of table 1 do; T61 waits for both;

## The analysis of E8

**R41. Under grade 5 the estimate comes to grade 3's later on average because of a tail, not because every learner comes slowly: 0.314 of the learners never come within the run of 200 answers, against 0.021 under grade 1, and those who come do so in 29.9 answers on average, against 28.9. The tail starts in the trial series. The engine gives its tasks a band higher under grade 5, as the model's start is, and the maximum of the series' posterior moves with the start and the tasks alike: a learner who answers the harder tasks as under grade 3 ends the series up to as far above grade 3's estimate as it started — 2.5 to the digit where its tasks were grade 3's a band up. Under grade 1 the module has no band below the first, the tasks of the series are grade 3's, and only the prior differs. After the series the step of θ — K_θ falling to 0.05, with δ taking much of each pull — closes such a gap slowly. σ₀ moves grade 1 alone; the step after the series and a longer series move grade 5, and at the base's three cells no set of them brings grade 5 to 0.90. Neither guessing nor forgetting is the cause of the tail.** Task T59 of epic E8 (R40, decision 1); its plan is the executor's, in a run of `spec`'s orchestrator (R156 spec).
- **The harness** is `sim` `v0.1.21`, commit bc9aaaf, whose bench `v0.1.22` keeps (sources 6.12), with the base of its `go.mod` — `learner-model` v0.1.8, `engine` v0.1.6 —, the cells `D-grade1`, `D-grade3` and `D-grade5` at 2,000 working learners each, and a trace of θ, P, P* and the task after each of the first 200 answers, built in the clone in the session's folder; the variants are commits on top of `main` e3aaee6 there. How to repeat the runs, the variants and the SHA-256 of every file are [measurements.md](measurements.md), section 5.1. On the base the trace gives `M11` of R58 sim to every digit, so the cells on `v0.1.21` are those of `fairness-1`. 12 runs of the three cells — one `sim run`, the base's trace and ten variants' — took from 2 to about 8 minutes each by the clock on 20 cores, the longer ones while the machine was busy with other work.
- **What the runs showed** (measurements 5.2, 5.3):
  1. *a tail.* The learners who never come carry 62.8 of grade 5's 83.33 answers, and 4.2 of grade 1's 32.52; the median is 30 answers under grade 5 and 16 under grade 1. Against the truth grade 5 is no worse than grade 3: |θ − θ*| after the 50th answer is 0.59 against 0.64, after the 200th 0.37 against 0.41 — but it is high, by +0.25 and +0.10, while grade 3 is at −0.04 and −0.07 and grade 1 at −0.41 and −0.30. `M11` measures whether the two estimates agree, and under grade 5 they disagree in both directions;
  2. *the trial series.* Its five tasks — one skill's difficulties 1, 3 and 5, then the next skill's 1 and 3 — are grade 3's under grade 1 for every learner, while under grade 5 the first skill is of band 2, not band 1, for every learner. The learner n answers by the same fractions under every grade (R55 sim), so under grade 5, on harder tasks, nobody has fewer wrong answers. Where the counts are equal — 0.201 of the learners — the series ends 1.7–2.5 above grade 3's estimate, and 0.958 of them never come; 288 learners whose five tasks were exactly the reference's a band up and who answered them alike end it at the reference plus 2.5 to every digit of the trace. Where grade 5 has two or three wrong answers and grade 3 none — 0.106 of the learners —, the series ends 1.4–3.1 below the reference, which stays high after five right answers on easy tasks, and 0.72 of them never come. Under grade 1 the same five answers end the series 0.42–0.80 below the reference;
  3. *after the series.* A gap of 2.5 is 1.5 after the 50th answer and 0.9 after the 200th. The step is K_θ · (S − P), K_θ = 0.2 / (1 + 0.05 · n) down to the floor 0.05 at n = 60, and the skills' δ, with K_δ = 0.4 / (1 + 0.05 · m) and m counted by skill, take much of each pull: under grade 5 the mean δ of the tasks' skills is −0.21 over answers 101–200 against the reference's −0.11. Without the step of δ — a diagnostic, K₀ of δ 0 — 0.180 of grade 5 never come instead of 0.314;
  4. *the variants.* σ₀ 3.0 brings grade 1 to 0.9135 and leaves grade 5 at 0.5725; σ₀ 2.0 and 4.0 are worse under both grades, 4.0 because θ in the series swings far enough for the engine to give grade 1 other tasks than grade 3. Under grade 5 the share of equal-series learners who never come stays 0.948–0.970 at σ₀ 2.0, 2.5 and 3.0: with the tasks moved by the start, a prior of any width moves with them. A series of weight 10, a floor of K_θ of 0.1 and K₀ of θ 0.4 bring grade 5's never to 0.188, 0.144 and 0.125 and its `M11.same` to 0.5945, 0.5655 and 0.669. σ₀ 3.0 with K₀ 0.4 and a series of 10 gives 0.936 under grade 1 and 0.735 under grade 5, never 0.073 — the most of the runs. The step without forgetting moves grade 5 a little — never 0.285 against 0.314 — and grade 1 the other way, `M11.same` 0.7945 against 0.812, and 0.928 of grade 5's equal-series learners still never come.
- **Why so.** In the series θ is the maximum of L(θ) = −(θ − θ₀)² / (2σ₀²) + Σ log-likelihood of the answers (architecture 3.1). When θ₀ and every βᵢ move by 2.5 and the outcomes stay, L moves by 2.5 and its maximum with it: the series cannot tell a start a band too high from the truth unless the answers differ. They differ only by the tasks being harder for the learner, and on three to five tasks one learner in five answers them as under the true band. The engine chooses the series' tasks by the model's P — a skill is within reach when a task of it has P ≥ 0.70, the corridor is 0.70–0.85 (`skills.go` of `engine` v0.1.6; sources 6.12) —, so the tasks follow the start; under grade 1 they cannot go below the first band, which is where grade 3's already are. After the series the gap is left to the step of Elo, whose K_θ is at most 0.16 at n = 5 and falls to the floor by n = 60, while each new skill's δ starts at 0 and takes the larger share of the pull. Grade 1's tail is the mirror of the same with the reference: a learner high above grade 3's start answers all of the easy series right, the likelihood of right answers has no maximum, and the prior keeps 0.80 of the 2.5 between the starts, against 0.42–0.51 after one or two wrong answers.
- **What does not explain it:**
  1. the guessing floor: no item of the series has guessing, and 20 % of the answers after it are a choice of five, alike under the grades;
  2. forgetting in the step: without it the tail of grade 5 is 0.285 instead of 0.314, and 0.928 of its equal-series learners still never come; it is no cause of the tail, though its share of the step after the series is for T61 to weigh with K₀;
  3. σ₀ alone: it reaches grade 1, whose tasks are the reference's, but not the equal-series learners of grade 5;
  4. the bands of the tasks after the 50th answer: `M11.band` holds (R58 sim).
- **What was decided:**
  1. the cause is the trial series with tasks that move with the start, which leaves one learner in five a band off, and the slow step of θ after it. Both are the model's to shorten, and the first is also the engine's choice: the row «в реестр spec» В52 of [run/QUESTIONS.md](../run/QUESTIONS.md) carries it to `engine` and `sim`, and the round goes on with what the model can (RUN.md, epic E8, «Не входит»);
  2. what goes into T61: the numbers of the step after the series — K₀ of θ (0.3–0.4) and its floor (0.075–0.1) —, the weight of the series (7.5–10), σ₀ 3.0 only together with them, and K₀ of δ below 0.4 as a lead; their pairs and the triple of measurements 5.3 as the first sets. Beside `M11.same`, `M11.answers` and `M11.band`, T61 reads the share that never comes and |θ − θ*| of all three cells — a set that brings the grades together by moving grade 3 off the truth is no fairer —, and the price of a wider step on the 65 other cells, which these runs did not measure (measurements 5.3);
  3. a start other than the grade's band is not measured here. The truth of the cells `D-` is on the middle band (R55 sim), so a start drawn toward the middle of the scale wins `M11` by construction, and T61 measures such a rule on the learners whose truth is off the middle band too before its row «в реестр spec» (R40, decision 3);
  4. the risk of T61 is sharper: at the base's three cells the most of these runs is 0.735 under grade 5, and what is left is the learners whose series repeats the true band's answers. If no set of the model's numbers holds 0.90, the exit of the round, T61's "Риски", comes with В52's answer.
- **Rejected:**
  - σ₀ alone as T61's set: it does not reach the cause under grade 5, and 2.0 and 4.0 are worse under both grades;
  - the step without forgetting and K₀ of δ 0 as candidates: the first barely moves `M11` and leaves the tail, and the second removes the skills' corrections, which R03 keeps;
  - runs of the other 65 cells here: the cause is in the three cells, and the price of the sets is T61's, at the size of the deciding run (R40, decision 1);
  - a trace of the engine's choice: the tasks the trace shows told the causes apart without it.

— *Consequence:* nothing changes in the code and the tests. [measurements.md](measurements.md), section 5; the amendments of 2026-11-16 to R40 and to R24; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 2.1, "Deferred"; RUN.md, T61, «Решить при планировании» and «Разделы»; [sources.md](sources.md), 6.12; [run/DEBT.md](../run/DEBT.md), Д5; [run/QUESTIONS.md](../run/QUESTIONS.md), В51 and В52; [plan/E8.md](plan/E8.md), T59. — R03, R24, R31, R40; R55, R56, R58 sim.

— *Amendment 2026-11-16 (T61; R42):* decision 2's sets are measured at the size of the deciding run: each lifts `M11.same` but K₀ of δ alone, which moves it within the noise, none of those checked holds every constraint, and grade 5 stays below 0.90 in all 48 sets screened — at most 0.853. Decision 4's risk came true: the round ends with the exit, and its long runs stop by the author's decision of 2026-11-16.

— *Second amendment 2026-11-16 (R44):* **В52 is answered by R157 spec: not the tasks of the trial series of `engine` — its option (a), which would leave the model's start where the asymmetry is —, but the start of the estimate a band below the band of the grade, in this library, by row 4.20 of `spec`'s `COORDINATION.md`.** — R44.

## The exploration of E8

**R42. The exploration T61 found no candidate for the judgement `model-fair-1`: every set of the model's numbers of the start and the step that was checked against "no worse" — 12 of the 48 screened, the strongest and the most promising of each kind — lifts `M11.same` of grades 1 and 5 and fails "no worse" elsewhere, and none brings grade 5 near the bound 0.90 — at the three cells `D-` the most of 48 sets is 0.853, and the strongest set run in full, `kb06n50w10`, fails 23 checks. The threshold 0.90 is not reached by fitting constants. By the author's decision of 2026-11-16 the long runs of fitting constants on `sim` stop until there are 100 children: T62 and T63 are deferred, `[?]`, and the round goes to its outcome, T64, with the numbers of this exploration. The base stays as it is.** Task T61 of epic E8 (R40), its plan the executor's in a run of `spec`'s orchestrator (R156 spec); the author's answer to В51 of 2026-11-16, changed the same day — about ten decisive runs, since T59 found two causes, the base first and a screening on the cells `D-` before them —, and the author's decision of 2026-11-16, given through `spec`'s coordinator in the run «Сделай эпик E…», which stopped the session (R156 spec), and recorded here first: no more long runs of fitting constants on `sim` while there are fewer than 100 children; the fast gate in CI stays, and so do one-off analyses of how the model works and rules in place of calibration — the estimate starts from below, an adult corrects the level, a guard lowers the level after N failures in a row. `spec` is to record it in its log on closing its task T171, and a task of `spec` then rewrites dpia 7 spec and dpia 8 spec; until then this entry is where it is written. Rejected by the author: carrying the round E8 to the bound 0.90.
- **The harness** is `sim` `v0.1.23`, commit 61ed988, the tag of "Gate" and of the criterion `model-fair-1` (the 2026-11-16 amendment to R20; R65, R66 sim), with the base of its `go.mod`, `learner-model` v0.1.8 and `engine` v0.1.6. The cells `D-` of the base give the bits of T59 — the SHA-256 of measurements 5.1 —, and its decisive run gives `base` of `sim`'s `paths.json` to the byte. The yardstick is `sim judge` by a judgement file of `model-fair-1` in the session's folder: the target, `fair` and every "no worse" of the model's criterion as is (R65 sim). How to repeat the runs, the variants, their commits and the SHA-256 of the files are [measurements.md](measurements.md), section 7; the variants are commits on top of `main` 26be23c in a clone in the session's folder and went nowhere.
- **The runs:** 48 sets screened on the three cells `D-` at 2,000 working learners; 9 sets on the twelve cells of risk, judged in a copy of the base's file; 5 decisive runs — the base, `k04w10`, `kb04n50w10`, `kb06n50w10` and `k04d02` —, within the author's budget of about ten. Two more were stopped unfinished, and one run of the cells of risk was not read, when the stopping rules or the author's decision came first (measurements 7.3, 7.4).
- **What the runs showed** (measurements 7.2–7.5):
  1. *the target moves.* K₀ of θ, the weight of the series and σ₀, alone or together, lift `M11.same` of both grades: K₀ of θ 0.4 and a series of 10, +0.124 [0.109; 0.139]; with K₀ 0.8, +0.205 and grade 5 at 0.853. The floor of K_θ moves the share that never comes, not `M11.same`, which reads the first 50 answers; a K_θ that decays faster, so as to be larger only right after the series, moves nothing or less — the gap is closed between n = 20 and n = 50. σ₀ 3.0 brings grade 1 to 0.91 and leaves grade 5 at 0.57, as T59 found (R41). None of the sets read moves grade 3 off the truth: its `M1` after the 200th answer is no worse than the base's (measurements 7.2);
  2. *the price moves with it.* Each of the four decisive candidates is better than the base by the target and fails 10 to 23 checks "no worse": `k04w10` 13, `kb04n50w10` 10, `kb06n50w10` 23, `k04d02` 18. A wider step of θ, even only below n = 50, breaks `M1.rms.200` on the cells of school maths and on `W-repeat` by up to 0.33 at the tolerance 0.01; at the cells of risk alone a series of 10 fails 6 checks, the start of `S-adult` among them, and σ₀ 3.0 fails 17, the error of the estimate and Brier;
  3. *`W-repeat`.* There the base's estimate is already +2.72 above the truth after the 200th answer: a repeat of a task whose solution has gone to the device weighs in full (architecture 3.3; Д7), and any wider step of θ adds to it. Moving the pull from δ to θ — K₀ of θ 0.4 and of δ 0.2, the sum 0.6 of the base — holds `W-repeat`, and the model's score is better than the base's, +0.051 [0.039; 0.062]; but δ that learns more slowly then breaks "mastered" late, `M6.never`, on five cells, the error on `G5`, `G0-skills1` and `S-scale3.5`, and the review dates `E3` on four cells of forgetting — 18 failures. A smaller sum breaks the jumps `G3` and `G3-drop`; a larger share of θ, 0.5 with 0.1, breaks `M6.never` and the start of `S-adult` on the cells of risk alone;
  4. *the bound.* Grade 1 passes 0.90 with K₀ of θ 0.5 and up at the base's decay of K_θ, or with σ₀ 3.0; grade 5 does not in any set, and the sets that come nearest are those that fail most. What T59 left — one learner in five whose series repeats the true band's answers on tasks a band up — is the engine's choice as much as the model's (R41; В52).
- **What was decided:**
  1. no candidate goes to `model-fair-1`, and T62 has nothing to draft: by "Риски" of T61, the round's exit. The author's decision of 2026-11-16 makes it so without a row of table 3: T62 and T63 are `[?]`, "the author's decision of 2026-11-16, long runs after 100 children", and T64, which `spec`'s coordinator starts, carries the numbers of this exploration to dpia 10.2 spec and dpia 8 spec;
  2. the threshold 0.90 of R55 sim is not reached by fitting the model's constants: at the cells `D-` grade 5 stays below it in all 48 sets, and every set that lifts it and was checked is paid for on the 65 other cells; the 36 sets not checked lie between those checked or beyond the strongest of them, so no unchecked set is likely to hold where its neighbours do not; The levers that are left are the rules the author named — the estimate starting from below, an adult's correction of the level, a guard after N failures in a row — and the engine's choice of the trial series' tasks (В52). Which of them this library takes, and how, is a decision of `spec` and of a task of its own here, not of this round;
  3. the facts worth keeping for whoever returns: the gap after the series is closed between n = 20 and n = 50, so a faster decay of K_θ is no lever; a wider step of θ meets the full weight of a repeat on `W-repeat` (Д7) and needs a smaller step of δ, which in turn delays "mastered"; a copy of the base's decisive file with a variant's lines on the cells of risk is a cheap and exact screening of "no worse" there, but no more — `k04d02` held them all but four and failed eighteen on the decisive run.
- **Rejected:**
  - more decisive runs — the author's decision of 2026-11-16, and by the stopping rules too: the sets left to measure are milder than `k04d02` or stronger than `kb06n50w10`, and the first lift less while the second fail more;
  - a start by the grade other than the grade's band: not measured. R51 spec keeps a learner in the band of their grade, a start drawn toward the middle wins `M11` on the cells `D-` by construction (R41, decision 3), and the author's rule "the estimate starts from below" is `spec`'s to write first;
  - a candidate with failures, as the best that can be had: the judgement's constraints are written down before the first candidate (R65 sim), and the judgement would repeat each failure number for number on the same learners (R32);
  - a rule for the weight of a repeat as part of this round: it is the weights' own question (architecture 2.3; Д7), with its own cause, and its first variant, the weight 1/2 of an answer with help, failed 62 checks (R32).

— *Consequence:* nothing changes in the code and the tests, and the documents are outside the release's `paths`: the merge releases no tag (R16). [measurements.md](measurements.md), section 7; the 2026-11-16 amendments to R24, R40 and R41; RUN.md, T62 and T63 `[?]`, and T61 in [plan/E8.md](plan/E8.md); [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 2.1, "Deferred"; [run/DEBT.md](../run/DEBT.md), Д5 and Д7; [run/QUESTIONS.md](../run/QUESTIONS.md), В51 left it. — R03, R24, R32, R40, R41; R55, R65 and R66 sim; T171 spec.

## The speed of CI and the tag v0.18.4 of `spec`

**R43. CI is faster by row 7.21 of `spec`'s `COORDINATION.md`, and what its checks prove does not change. `ci-test` runs the tests twice, each a check of its own: `go test -race` and then the run of the coverage with `-covermode=set`. "Gate" is four jobs "Gate / part k", each `sim`'s `just gate-part k 4`, and after them the check "Gate", `just gate-check bands`, on `ubuntu-24.04-arm`, with `sim`'s cache of Go, on `sim` `v0.1.25`. The shared workflows are `.github` v0.1.31, and "Proto" takes the image by the steps of its `go.yml`. `spec` is v0.18.4, and `.devcontainer/Dockerfile` has the stage `toolchain-core`, which `toolchain` adds nothing to.** Decided by the executor 2026-11-16 in T66, in a run «Сделай эпик E…» of `spec`'s orchestrator (R156 spec), by row 7.21 and the sixth 2026-11-15 amendment to R76 spec, which leaves a change of what a check proves to the repository; the coordinator's word of 2026-11-16 (T171 spec): rows 7.13 and 7.14 are out — `.github` v0.1.31 and `spec` v0.18.4 —, so the row goes whole, with the move to both, and the author agreed to the change of `.devcontainer/`. How it is built is the executor's: no document reserves it for the author.
- **What changes:**
  - `justfile`, `ci-test` — `go test -race -count=1 ./...`, then `go test -count=1 -covermode=set -coverpkg=./... -coverprofile=coverage/go.out ./...`, in place of one run with `-race` and `-covermode=atomic`. Go takes `atomic` by itself under `-race`, "significantly more expensive", and `set` says whether a statement ran (sources 5.8), which is all `codecov/patch` reads. Both still run in "Go / test" and "Go / arm64";
  - "Gate" — four jobs "Gate / part k" and the check "Gate", by the jobs of `engine`'s T66 (R17 engine, its third 2026-11-16 amendment) and `sim`'s recipes (sources 6.14): each part writes its share of the learners into a folder that goes on as an artifact, and "Gate" merges the four, holds the build to `gate.json` and the numbers to their bands, with the same table and exit as `just gate`, and refuses a part missing, repeated or of another run. "Gate" runs when a part failed too, `!cancelled()`, and then fails. Each part checks the tag against the SHA first, by the step "sim tag", so a wrong pair stops in seconds; `SIM_TAG` and `SIM_COMMIT` move to the `env` of the workflow, named once for all five jobs. All five run on `ubuntu-24.04-arm`, since a part splits its share by the runner's processors. The cache of Go — `GOCACHE` and `GOMODCACHE` — is the steps of `sim`'s `gate.yml` word for word: keyed by `sim/go.sum` and `sim/.devcontainer/Dockerfile`, with `restore-keys`, and part 0 saves it on a push to `main` alone; its builds are touched after the restore, as `go.yml` touches its own (R41 .github), so that Go does not delete a build of an entry older than five days at the end of the first command. The image of `sim`'s stage comes by the steps of the job `arm64` of `go.yml` v0.1.31, keyed by `sim`'s Dockerfile, saved by part 0 on a push to `main`. The limit is 15 minutes a job, as in `sim`'s `gate.yml`;
  - `sim` `v0.1.25`, commit 0a31313, in place of `v0.1.23`: the latest tag and the first with `gate.yml` for callers. `gate.json`, `go.sum` and `.devcontainer/` are those of `v0.1.23` (sources 6.14), so the base — `learner-model` v0.1.8 at ab688b2 and `engine` v0.1.6 —, the 69 bands and the image are the same, and so is what a candidate's gate means;

## The three rules of R157 spec

**R45. The model holds the three rules of R157 spec in place of a calibration. A module's level starts a band's width below the band of the learner's grade, below the lowest band too. An adult's correction — evidence of kind 3, which `LevelCorrection` gives — sets the level at the middle of the band the adult chose, in one step, and keeps it when the level stands in that band already; either way it ends the trial series and starts the guard's count anew. Three failures of the module in a row past its trial series lower the level by a band: a failure is a piece of evidence on which the model expected the learner to know at least half of the answers, above the guessing floor, and fewer than half of the items with an answer were solved at the end of the attempt. `Read` gives the band the level stands in. The state is of kind 2.** T68, a task outside an epic, written by the text the orchestrator gave for row 4.20 of `spec`'s `COORDINATION.md` (rule 6 of `COORDINATION.md` spec), with the addition of T178 spec on `Read`, in the run of the whole «Порядок» by the orchestrator, the author's word of 2026-11-16 (R156 spec). The author decided where each rule lives and its numbers — a band below, a band in one step, three failures (R157 spec and its 2026-11-16 amendment); the task leaves what a failure is to this log, and the rest is the execution's.
- **The start** (the 2026-11-16 amendment to R03). `BandByGrade` gives the band of the grade, as before, and the evidence carries it as the start band and the state pins it: the band the start is counted from (the 2026-11-16 amendment to R51 spec). θ₀ = 2.5 · (p − 1) − 2.5, so a learner of the lowest band's grades, or with no grade, starts at −2.5, the place below the lowest band that R03's scale had not. The trial series centres its prior there, and `Readout.Task` reads a module with no evidence from there.
- **The band the level stands in**, `ModuleReadout.LevelBand` with `HasLevelBand`: the band of the module's current version whose middle task, at 2.5 · (p − 1), is nearest the level, within half a band, the upper of two as near; the highest band when the level is above it; none when the level is more than 1.25 below the lowest band's middle, where the lowest band's learners start, and none without a current version or bands. For a module with no evidence it is the band of the starting point. It is the `level_band` of the skill map and what the check of `SetModuleLevel` holds the band of a correction next to (T178 spec).
- **The correction**, `LevelCorrection(module, band, start, at)`: kind 3 adds `level_set_by_adult`, field 21. Its record names no task — `task_id` `<module>/level/<band ID>`, the band set as `task_band`, difficulty 3, the adult as both provenances — and holds no skills, items, traps or other facts of an attempt, and the codec and `Append` refuse a correction other than it gives. `Append`: θ becomes the band's offset unless it stands in the band set already; the trial series ends if it lasts (`TrialEndedByAdult`, its answers cleared), the module's last evidence is not in it, and the count of the guard is 0, so that a repeat changes nothing. No skill moves, n stays, and its time is no practice. A correction as a module's first evidence creates the module, with the evidence's start band. A band above the current version's highest sets the level at the highest, so that a correction logged on another version still folds (R08), and a band is read by its place, as an attempt's is; a module with no current version waits, as with any evidence. The library does not check that the band is next to the current one: `api`'s method does (T178 spec), and a log that `wrong_key` cut (R63 spec) may put a correction after another level than the one it was made at, which the recomputation must still read.
- **The guard.** Past the trial series, a piece of evidence that moves the module is a failure when the sum of R̄ · σ of its answers — their P above the guessing floor, from the state before it, as the step takes them — is at least half their number, and fewer than half of the items with an answer were solved at the end: the final verdict right, or the first with no final one recorded; an item "not solved" by a shown solution is not. `FailuresInARow` of the module counts them; the third lowers θ by 2.5 after the step, but not below −2.5 nor upward from below it; the count goes on, lowering no more, until a piece of evidence that is no failure starts it at 0. The trial series counts nothing, and a correction starts the count anew.
- **The state** is of kind 2: `failures_in_a_row` and `trial_ended_by_adult`, fields 9 and 10 of a module, written from kind 2, required there and refused in kind 1. `api` recomputes its cache by the new version anyway (R08; R80 spec). Evidence is of kind 3.

— *Why:*
- **The start band stays the grade's.** Every log then starts its modules by the new rule on recomputation, the evidence keeps one meaning for the start band, and no place 0 enters the evidence, whose places start at 1 (R08). R51 spec counts the start from the grade's band.
- **The failure is the model's own expectation and the end of the attempt**, not R06's fewer than half right at the first verdict. In the corridor a learner at its level fails about 22.5 % of first answers (the target 0.775), and three in a row come to 36 % of such learners within 50 answers and to 84 % within 200 — a computation of runs, measurements 9.1 —, against the 5 % of row 4.21 of `spec`'s `COORDINATION.md`. On `sim`'s gate, "stuck at the end" alone fired 12,886 times, and `M1.rms.200` of `G0` in olympiad maths went from 0.50 to 1.50: most of the attempts stuck on were tasks the model itself already gave a P near 0, far below the corridor of `engine` (measurements 9.2). Such a task says nothing of the level. A task the model gave as fitting that the learner could not solve even on the ladder says that the level stands too high, which is what R157 spec gives the guard for.
- **Above the guessing floor.** P is c + (1 − c) · R̄ · σ, never below c, so with P a choice of two, c = ½, would be fitting however far above the level; R̄ · σ is the chance the learner knows the answer, and with no guessing it is P.
- **One lowering a run.** Without it a learner stuck on a run of tasks that stay hard for it sinks a band each three to the floor.
- **The middle of the band, the nearest band.** The middle task of a band, difficulty 3, stands at its offset (R03), so the correction puts the level where the band's tasks are in the corridor, and the band it stands in is then the one set; the nearest middle makes "Easier" from a level near the next band's middle a step of about a band, not of almost two.
- **The correction ends the series.** The series finds the start (R03); the adult gave it. Left on, the series' next answer would compute θ anew from θ₀ and undo the correction.
- **The form of the record.** R08 holds that an earlier version reads a new form: one before kind 3 skips field 21 and refuses evidence whose task names no module, so the record carries a task ID that names the module and nothing an earlier version reads as an answer, and it moves nothing there.

— *Rejected:*
- a failure by R06's first verdict, one "stuck at the end" without the model's expectation, and the expectation by P with its guessing floor — above;
- a correction to a band the current version does not have refused — a log written on a version with more bands could no longer be recomputed;
- a start band a band lower in the evidence, `BandByGrade` giving the band below or a place 0 below the lowest — the start band would mean two things, a place 0 changes the checks of the evidence, and the logs before kind 3 would keep the former start on recomputation;
- a correction that keeps the series, its centre moved to the band set — a new field of the state, and five more answers would move the level a band either way, against "in one step";
- a record with a field of its own for the module and no task — an earlier version refuses it, against R08;
- the adjacency of the band in the library — above;
- a guard with no floor — a learner stuck on everything would sink without bound.

— *Consequence:* R03, R06, R08 and the 2026-11-16 amendments to R10 and R44; [architecture.md](architecture.md), the status, sections 1, 2.1, 3.1, 3.3, 3.5 and 3.7; [measurements.md](measurements.md), 9; CLAUDE.md and README.md; [run/DEBT.md](../run/DEBT.md), Д20 closed; the golden files of `testdata/` written anew, a new golden log `rules` and the golden correction `testdata/wire/correction.binpb`. What the model computes changes, so "Gate", on the bands of `learner-model` v0.1.8, is red until `sim` records the bands of this change: [run/QUESTIONS.md](../run/QUESTIONS.md), В54. A rough look of `sim` names no failure of `fair` (R57 sim), and many "no worse" checks fail by the start (measurements 9.3). — T68 ([plan/E8.md](plan/E8.md)); `rules.go`, `model.go`, `evidence.go`, `state.go`, `readout.go`, the codec; `proto/`.

— *Amendment 2026-11-17:* **`sim` judged the three rules at the commit of the pull request, df338e5, and the gate stands on its tag `v0.1.28` with the bands recorded on that commit (the 2026-11-17 amendment to R20).** The author's answer to В54: row 4.21 of `spec`'s `COORDINATION.md` ran on the commit of the pull request, then `sim` cut the tag and this pull request raised `SIM_TAG` and `SIM_COMMIT`. On 2,000 learners a cell the short run of R72 `sim` held two of its four conditions — `M11.same` of grade 5, 0.812 against the base's 0.5525, and the guard's false firings of grade 3, 0.0005 against at most 0.05 — and not `fair`, one of its six checks, nor the correction against `D-grade1`; the correction held against the truth (R73 `sim`). The author took the failure of `fair` into the residual risk 7 of dpia spec (R74 `sim`). The rough look of measurements 9.3, on 300 learners, did not see it.

## The context of E8's sessions

**R47. E8's sessions carried more context a call than E7's because they were longer, not because of the documents: over each session's first 80 main calls E8 carries 122.1 thousand a call, 4.5 % below the 127.9 of E7's five sessions, and its three sessions of 111, 138 and 234 main calls are the whole excess over the reference. What entered from the documents is E7's in size. So `docs-budgets.json`, the order of reading of rule 2 of RUN.md and the documents stay as they are; what R145 spec's threshold and the waits for long runs need goes to `spec`, as В56 and В57.** Task T65 outside an epic, set by the threshold of R145 spec that section 4 of [measurements.md](measurements.md) found for E8 (the third 2026-11-16 amendment to R40); its decisions are the executor's, in a run of `spec`'s orchestrator (R156 spec).
- **What was measured** (measurements 10, 2026-11-17): `tokens-report` over the windows of E7 and E8 and a script over the same transcripts for each main call by its number in the session. Every session starts at 26–40 thousand and grows over its first 60 calls by 1.7–3.0 thousand a call, E8's no faster than E7's; E7's longest has 79 main calls. What entered between calls by Bash is 1.28 million characters in E8 against 1.26 in E7, `docs/decisions.md` 294,693 against 293,746 by Bash, and RUN.md less; it carried more only because it rode on more calls after it. T58 wrote the epic and measured E7, T59 and T61 ran `sim`'s cells, and T61 waited for 19 background runs in 76 main calls, $3.08 of its $14.62.
- **What was decided:**
  1. `docs-budgets.json` is neither raised nor lowered: the documents brought into E8's sessions what they brought into E7's, and a lower limit would cut what a task has to read, not the length that cost;
  2. rule 2 of RUN.md keeps its order: its reads come at the start of a session, where E8 is below E7;
  3. the length of a task stays rule 11's to judge. T59 and T61 were long by their runs — T61 by its 48 sets screened and decided (R42) —, a split of an exploration into sessions brings each the documents again, and nothing here measures that it would be cheaper;
  4. the waits are the shared rules' to fix, not one repository's: В57. Until `spec` answers, nothing here says how to wait;
  5. the threshold's mean tells the length of the sessions from the documents only by a figure that `tokens-report` does not print: В56.
- **Rejected:**
  - lower limits for `docs/decisions.md`'s entries or the sections of [architecture.md](architecture.md), or a split of the log: neither brought E8 more than E7, and an entry is never rewritten;
  - a sentence on waiting in RUN.md or in a rule of `.claude/rules/` here: no part's `paths` matches the temporary clone where an exploration runs `sim`, RUN.md keeps the template's rules but where R39 holds, and a sentence of RULES.md spec reaches every repository whose task runs long;
  - E8 as a new reference: the reference is this repository's first row of at least 300 main calls after T50, E7's 128.2 (measurements 1.9 spec), and a threshold met by length is no reason to move it.

— *Consequence:* nothing changes in the code, the tests, CI, the container or the budgets. [measurements.md](measurements.md), sections 4 and 10; the fifth amendment 2026-11-17 to R40; [run/QUESTIONS.md](../run/QUESTIONS.md), В56 and В57; T65 ([plan/E8.md](plan/E8.md)). — R40; R145 spec, R155 spec; measurements 1.5 spec, measurements 1.9 spec.
